### [1] [Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning for Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09958)
*Ming Cheng,Hao Chen,Ziyi Yang,Ziluowen Luo,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UAMP框架，通过不确定性感知的人车意图预测和不确定性校准的价值学习，提升混合交通场景下自动驾驶的运动规划安全性与舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的方法将人类意图预测视为确定性状态直接纳入观测，忽略了人类意图固有的不确定性（行为多样性、感知噪声、部分可观测性），可能导致自动驾驶车辆做出不安全决策。

Method: 1) 邻近感知不确定性估计器（Proximity-aware Uncertainty Estimator）量化交互条件化的意图不确定性；2) 构建不确定性引导的联合意图分布；3) 不确定性校准价值学习（UCVL）修正因直接引入不确定意图预测导致的价值函数学习偏差。

Result: 在多种混合交通场景下的大量实验表明，UAMP相比现有方法显著提升了安全性和驾驶舒适性，同时保持了交通效率。

Conclusion: 在混合交通环境中，显式建模和校准人类意图预测中的不确定性，对于自动驾驶的安全决策至关重要，UAMP为实现这一目标提供了有效的框架。

Abstract: In mixed-traffic environments where autonomous and human-driven vehicles may co-exist, motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires anticipating the future behaviors of surrounding human drivers. Existing reinforcement learning-based methods generally directly incorporate the predicted human intents into the observation to enable a proactive planning. However, human intent is inherently uncertain due to the behavioral diversity, perception noise, and partial observability. Treating predicted intends as deterministic states can result in unsafe decisions for autonomous vehicles. To address this problem, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning (UAMP), which incorporates uncertainty in human intent prediction for AV decision-making. Specifically, UAMP first introduces a proximity-aware uncertainty estimator to quantify the interaction-conditioned intent uncertainty and constructs an uncertainty-guided joint intent distribution over surrounding human-driven vehicles. Within this uncertainty set, UAMP further introduces Uncertainty-Calibrated Value Learning (UCVL) to correct value function learning biases arising from directly incorporating uncertain human intent predictions into the observation. Extensive experiments in various mixed-traffic scenarios show that UAMP significantly improves safety and driving comfort, while maintaining traffic efficiency compared with existing approaches. The code is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UAMP-5638.

</details>


### [2] [LieIPM: Lie Group Interior Point Method for Direct Trajectory Optimization of Rigid Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10579)
*Sangli Teng,Ruiqi Zhang,Tzu-Yuan Lin,William A Clark,Mark Mueller,Ram Vasudevan,Maani Ghaffari,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩阵李群的结构感知约束轨迹优化框架，利用二阶刚体模型和李群内点法（LieIPM）实现无奇异性的刚体运动规划，数值实验表明该方法相比通用求解器具有更强的鲁棒性和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束优化器通常在欧氏空间运行，忽略了刚体运动的流形结构，导致奇异性或病态优化问题。为了弥合这一差距，需要开发一种能够直接处理矩阵李群上流形结构的轨迹优化方法。

Method: 基于二阶刚体模型的李群结构，设计牛顿型更新以保持几何结构；提出线搜索李群内点法（LieIPM）处理流形上的约束；利用李群变分积分器实例化刚体运动规划框架，并推导利用群对称性的闭式内蕴导数。

Result: LieIPM通过构造保留了旋转运动的拓扑结构并避免了奇异性；数值结果表明，该方法在鲁棒性和收敛速度上优于通用求解器和结构利用型最优控制方法。

Conclusion: 结构感知的李群轨迹优化方法能有效解决刚体运动规划中的几何一致性问题，具有更好的数值性能和理论保障。

Abstract: Designing dynamically feasible trajectories for rigid bodies is a fundamental problem in robotics. While direct methods are widely used, the existing constrained optimizers typically operate in Euclidean space and ignore the manifold structure of rigid body motions. This mismatch may introduce singularities or lead to poorly conditioned optimization problems. To bridge this gap, we develop a structure-aware framework for constrained trajectory optimization directly on matrix Lie groups. Our approach is based on the second-order rigid body models utilizing Lie group structures, which enables efficient Newton-type updates while preserving the underlying geometry. Building on this model, we propose a line-search Lie Group Interior Point Method (LieIPM) to handle constraints on the manifolds. We instantiate the framework for rigid body motion planning using Lie group variational integrators and derive closed-form intrinsic derivatives that exploit group symmetries. The LieIPM preserves the topology of rotation motions by construction and avoids singularities. Numerical results demonstrate superior robustness and faster convergence compared to general-purpose solvers and structure-exploiting optimal control methods.

</details>


### [3] [GHOST: Hierarchical Sub-Goal Policies for Generalizing Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10025)
*Sriram Krishna,Ben Eisner,Haotian Zhan,Ying Yuan,Haoyu Zhen,Chuang Gan,Shubham Tulsiani,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GHOST框架，通过将操作策略分解为高层子目标预测（基于多视角RGB-D观测预测3D末端执行器位姿分布）和低层目标条件控制器，并利用空间接口将3D目标投影为图像平面的热图，实现了超越训练分布的泛化。该层次化分解在多个操作任务中优于扁平扩散策略，且能利用人类示范视频训练高层策略，实现跨具身迁移和小样本适应新物体与任务变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动操作策略泛化能力有限，难以应对训练分布外的场景；扁平策略（如Diffusion Policy）在复杂任务中鲁棒性不足，且人类示范数据因动作重定向噪声难以有效利用。

Method: 提出GHOST框架：(1) 高层策略从多视角RGB-D观测预测3D末端执行器位姿分布作为子目标；(2) 低层目标条件控制器执行具身特定动作；(3) 设计空间接口将3D目标投影到图像平面形成末端执行器热图，实现基于图像策略的3D目标条件化；(4) 高层策略使用人类视频训练，低层策略使用机器人数据训练。

Result: 在多个操作任务上，GHOST的层次化分解一致优于扁平Diffusion Policy，性能和鲁棒性均有提升；该层次化接口便于融入人类示范，无需依赖有噪声的动作重定向；仅需少量人类示范即可适应新物体和任务变体。

Conclusion: 通过解耦子目标规划与具体执行，层次化策略结构可显著提升泛化能力，同时利用人类视频数据高效迁移技能，为少样本适应新任务场景提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present GHOST, a framework for learning visuomotor manipulation policies that generalize beyond the training distribution. GHOST factorizes control into (i) a high-level policy that predicts the next sub-goal as a distribution over 3D end-effector poses from multi-view RGB-D observations, and (ii) a low-level goal-conditioned controller that executes embodiment-specific actions. To condition image-based policies on 3D goals, we introduce a simple spatial interface that projects predicted goals into the image plane and represents them as end-effector heatmaps. Across a suite of manipulation tasks, this hierarchical factorization consistently improves performance and robustness compared to a flat Diffusion Policy.
  Further, we show that this hierarchical interface also makes it easy to incorporate human demonstrations without relying on (noisy) action retargeting. As sub-goals are largely embodiment-agnostic, we train the high-level policy on human video to specify how learned skills should be applied and composed, while keeping the low-level policy trained purely on robot data. This hierarchy enables adaptation to novel objects and task variations using a small number of human demonstrations.

</details>


### [4] [Robotic Nonprehensile Object Transportation with a Hanging Tray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10039)
*Adam Heins,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用绳索悬挂托盘（三维摆）替代刚性夹持托盘来解决机器人服务员问题，仅需3自由度移动基座即可实现摆运动带来的减滑减晃效果，并通过仿真和实物实验验证了有效性，最后集成了视觉交互演示系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人服务员问题中，刚性夹持托盘在运输时会产生较大剪切力，导致物体滑动和液体晃荡。虽然已有研究通过机械臂末端模拟摆运动来缓解此问题，但需要6自由度机械臂，成本高、复杂度大。本文旨在用更简单的硬件（3自由度移动基座+真实悬挂托盘）获得相同益处。

Method: 采用绳索将托盘悬挂于机器人末端执行器下方，使其成为三维摆；仅使用3自由度移动基座驱动，通过基座运动控制悬挂托盘的摆动力学；结合计算机视觉识别举手人员，通过视觉伺服引导机器人靠近。

Result: 仿真和实物实验表明，悬挂托盘在减少物体滑动和液体晃荡方面显著优于刚性夹持托盘；成功集成了基于视觉的交互式机器人服务员演示系统。

Conclusion: 悬挂托盘方案能以更低的硬件要求（3自由度移动基座而非6自由度机械臂）实现摆运动对运输稳定性的提升，为机器人服务员任务提供了更经济实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider the nonprehensile object transportation task known as the waiter's problem, in which a robot must move an object balanced on a tray from one location to another. In contrast to prior works on the robotic waiter's problem, which make the robot tilt a tray rigidly held by its end effector (EE), we use a tray suspended from the EE by ropes, such that it behaves like a three-dimensional pendulum. Some prior works have actuated the robot so that the EE simulates the behavior of a pendulum, because pendular motion reduces the shear forces acting on the transported objects, minimizing the sliding of rigid objects and sloshing in containers of liquid. In contrast, our use of a real hanging tray allows us to obtain the benefits of pendular motion while only actuating a 3 degree-of-freedom (DOF) mobile base, rather than requiring a full 6-DOF manipulator arm. Our experiments in simulation and on real hardware show that the hanging tray substantially reduces both sliding and sloshing compared to a static, rigidly-grasped tray. Furthermore, we integrate the hanging tray into an interactive robot waiter demonstration, which uses computer vision to identify people with a raised hand and visual servoing to steer toward them and allow them to access the tray.

</details>


### [5] [Efficient-WAM: A 1B-Parameter World-Action Model with Low-Cost Future Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10040)
*Jiajun Li,Tiecheng Guo,Yifan Ye,Rongyu Zhang,Xiaowei Chi,Qianpu Sun,Ying Li,Yunfan Lou,Yan Huang,Zhihe Lu,Meng Guo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Efficient-WAM 通过紧凑视频专家、令牌稀疏视频潜变量和非对称视频-动作去噪，在保持控制性能的同时，将世界-动作模型的推理延迟降低约30倍（至约100ms），实现了高效的机器人实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）依赖逼真的未来预测，导致高推理延迟，难以实现实时机器人部署。需要设计一种更高效的WAM，保留未来视觉预测的控制优势同时降低推理成本。

Method: 1) 从WAN-2.2-5B迁移的紧凑视频专家；2) 令牌稀疏视频潜变量；3) 非对称视频-动作去噪，为视频分配比动作更少的采样步数；将未来视频预测视为动作生成的紧凑引导信号而非追求视觉保真度。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上，1B参数模型在保持竞争性控制能力的同时，将每块推理延迟降至约100ms，比现有WAMs实现30倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过牺牲未来预测的视觉保真度换取推理效率是可行的，Efficient-WAM证明了未来视觉预测可以作为动作生成的紧凑引导信号，而非必须追求高视觉质量。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control by coupling future visual prediction with action generation. However, most existing WAMs rely on photorealistic future prediction, which incurs high inference latency and makes real-time robot deployment difficult. This motivates a more efficient WAM design that preserves the control benefits of future visual prediction while reducing its inference cost. We introduce Efficient-WAM, a World-Action Model that reduces the cost of future imagination while preserving its control benefit. Efficient-WAM improves inference efficiency via a compact video expert transferred from WAN-2.2-5B, token-sparse video latents, and asymmetric video-action denoising that allocates fewer sampling steps to video than to actions. Instead of optimizing the future branch for visual fidelity, Efficient-WAM treats future video prediction as a compact guidance signal for action generation. Comprehensive experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that Efficient-WAM maintains strong action performance despite visibly coarse future predictions. While maintaining competitive control capabilities, our 1B-parameter model can reduce per-chunk latency to around 100 ms during physical deployment, achieving a 30x speedup over existing WAMs.

</details>


### [6] [A Spiking Neural Architecture for Coordinating Arm and Locomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11034)
*Lea Steffen,Kathryn Simone,Graeme Damberger,Travis DeWolf,Hudson Ly,Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个将基于脉冲的臂控与双足 locomotion 集成于全尺寸仿真人形机器人的脉冲神经网络架构，通过神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA）实现力控臂运动与双足行走的协调，并利用脉冲基底节模型进行高层动作选择，在 Nengo 与 Isaac Sim 联合仿真中验证了目标抓取、数字绘制、路径跟踪及行走与臂控切换等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SNN的电机控制系统将双足 locomotion 和臂控分开处理，缺乏二者的集成控制方案，无法实现完整人形机器人的协调运动控制。

Method: 采用神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA）构建脉冲神经网络，分别实现力控臂控制和双足行走控制；高层动作选择由生物启发式的脉冲基底节模型介导；通过Nengo（神经控制器）与Isaac Sim（物理仿真环境）的联合仿真验证系统。

Result: 成功实现了目标抓取、连续数字绘制、路径跟踪行走以及通过基底节去抑制在行走与臂控之间的切换。

Conclusion: 这是首个将双足 locomotion 和臂控集成于全尺寸人形平台的脉冲控制器，全脉冲实现使其未来可部署于低功耗神经形态硬件。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) coupled with neuromorphic hardware offer energy-efficient solutions for humanoid robot control. However, existing SNN-based motor control systems address bipedal locomotion and arm control in isolation, leaving integrated control of both unaddressed. We present a spiking architecture that coordinates force-based arm control and bipedal locomotion in a simulated humanoid, using the Neural Engineering Framework (NEF) and Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA). High-level action selection between locomotor and arm control is mediated by a biologically grounded spiking basal ganglia model. We validate the system through co-simulation of Nengo, for the neural control, and Isaac Sim, demonstrating successful target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and finally, switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated spiking controller to combine bipedal locomotion and arm control on a full-scale humanoid platform. The full spike-based implementation enables future deployment on low-power neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [7] [Gradient based Bilevel for Inverse Optimal Control, a Riemannian approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10841)
*Ahmed-Manaf Dahmani,Vincent Bonnet,David Daney,François Charpillet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形的逆最优控制方法（RIOC），通过将最优轨迹的可行集建模为流形来解决传统方法计算昂贵和数值不稳定的问题，实验表明在保证重建精度的同时计算时间减少约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆最优控制（IOC）方法依赖双层优化，计算代价高；近期投影方法存在数值不稳定性，原因是标准约束规范被违反。

Method: 将满足最优性条件的轨迹集合建模为流形，提出黎曼逆最优控制（RIOC）方法，将观测轨迹投影到该流形上，在保持可行性的同时进行优化。

Result: 在真实人类手臂轨迹数据上，RIOC方法达到了与经典双层IOC相当或更好的重建精度，同时计算时间减少了约4倍。

Conclusion: 几何优化方法有望提升IOC在机器人和人类运动分析中的可扩展性和可靠性。

Abstract: Inverse Optimal Control (IOC) aims to recover the cost function that explains observed trajectories as solutions of an optimal control problem. Classical IOC formulations rely on bilevel optimization, which repeatedly solves a nested optimal control problem and quickly becomes computationally prohibitive for realistic systems. Recent projection-based approaches offer a promising alternative but suffer from numerical instability when solved with gradient-based methods due to violations of standard constraint qualifications.
  In this paper, we show that these difficulties stem from the geometric structure of the IOC feasible set. We demonstrate that the set of trajectories satisfying the optimality conditions naturally forms a manifold and reformulate IOC as an optimization problem on this manifold. Based on this insight, we propose a Riemannian Inverse Optimal Control (RIOC) method that projects observed trajectories onto the manifold of optimal solutions while preserving feasibility by construction. Experiments on real human arm trajectories show that the proposed method achieves comparable or better reconstruction accuracy than classical bilevel IOC while reducing computation time by about a factor of four. These results highlight the potential of geometric optimization methods to improve the scalability and reliability of IOC for robotics and human motion analysis.

</details>


### [8] [Flow Control: Steering Vision-Language-Action Models with Simple Real-Time Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10180)
*Jonathan C. Kao,Jason Chan,Andy Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"流控制"（flow control）的方法，通过通用输入（如键盘）实时引导视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的动作输出，无需重新训练或微调，即可将用户意图转化为高质量的动作样本，显著提升任务成功率和完成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在执行任务时缺乏实时用户干预能力，导致当模型行为与用户意图不一致时难以纠正。作者希望设计一种即插即用、无需重新训练的方法，允许用户通过简单输入实时引导VLA动作，同时保持动作的高质量和保真度。

Method: 流控制方法通过接收通用输入（如键盘信号），将这些粗粒度的用户输入转换为从VLA专家动作分布中采样的动作样本。该方法不改变VLA模型本身，而是通过控制动作采样过程来融合用户意图与模型学到的专家分布，实现实时动作引导。

Result: 实验表明流控制具备多项优势：(1) 能够准确且响应迅速地引导机器人动作；(2) 对次优用户输入具有鲁棒性；(3) 显著提升了任务成功率和完成速度；(4) 在流控制轨迹上微调VLA可提升自主策略性能。

Conclusion: 流控制提供了一种简单直观的实时人机交互方式，用户无需专业知识即可有效引导VLA模型的行为，从而提升任务表现。该方法无需额外训练成本，并具有即插即用的实用性。

Abstract: We introduce flow control of vision-language-action (VLA) models, a simple and effective way to steer VLA actions in real-time through generic inputs, such as a keyboard. This method can be used out-of-the-box and does not require retraining or fine-tuning VLAs. It enables relatively crude user inputs to steer a VLA to align with user intent. The VLA transforms these inputs into action samples drawn from the VLA expert action distribution learned during training, so that the generated actions are high quality (conformity to the action expert distribution) and high fidelity (reflecting the user's intent). We demonstrate that flow control has many desirable properties: (1) flow control accurately and responsively steers robot actions with user inputs, (2) it is robust to suboptimal user inputs, (3) it enables users to steer VLAs to achieve significantly higher success rates and faster task completion, and (4) fine-tuning a VLA on flow control trajectories improves the autonomous policy. Together, these results provide a simple and intuitive way for users to help steer VLA actions, increasing task performance.

</details>


### [9] [Resilient Navigation for Autonomous Farm Robots by Leveraging Jerk-Augmented Models with IMU-Only Disturbance Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10971)
*Batu Candan,Mohammed Atallah,Simone Servadio,Saeed Arabi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加加速度（jerk）增强扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）与多调谐因子（MTF）自适应方法相结合的鲁棒导航算法，用于应对农业自主机器人在GNSS/LiDAR/视觉传感器中断和非道路环境高频振动下的状态估计问题。通过动态调整测量协方差矩阵，该方法在Salin247自主机器人真实数据上显著降低了3D位置RMSE，提升了纯惯性推算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业自主机器人在非道路环境中常面临传感器中断（如GNSS/LiDAR/视觉）和高频振动，导致标准EKF基于恒定测量噪声假设的导航状态估计精度不足，亟需一种能自适应处理突发扰动和传感器异常值的鲁棒估计算法。

Method: 采用加加速度增强的扩展卡尔曼滤波（Jerk-Augmented EKF），并集成多调谐因子（MTF）自适应方法，实时动态调整测量协方差矩阵，以应对传感器中断和突发扰动。

Result: 在Salin247自主机器人真实数据上，该方法相比基线EKF模型显著降低了3D位置RMSE，展现出更优的纯惯性推算（dead-reckoning）能力。

Conclusion: 加加速度增强与MTF自适应相结合的策略能有效提升农业自主机器人在传感器中断和振动环境下的导航鲁棒性和状态估计精度。

Abstract: Precise state estimation for navigation of autonomous agricultural robots is often compromised by sensor outages (GNSS/LiDAR/Visual) and high-frequency vibrations inherent in off-road environments. This paper proposes a robust navigation algorithm based on a jerk-augmented Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with a Multiple Tuning Factor (MTF) adaptation method. Unlike standard EKF approaches that assume constant measurement noise, our method dynamically adjusts the measurement covariance matrix in real-time, allowing the system to cope with sudden disturbances and sensor outliers. We evaluate the algorithm using real-world data from a Salin247 autonomous robot. Results demonstrate that jerk-augmentation combined with MTF adaptation significantly reduces 3D position Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) compared to baseline EKF models, providing superior dead-reckoning capabilities.

</details>


### [10] [Exploration of Foundation Model-Based Robots in Patient and Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10208)
*Zhiwen Qiu,Wei Liu,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于基础模型的护理机器人在设计特征、用户体验和护理效果方面的现状，指出当前系统以语音社交辅助形态为主，多模态基础能力和物理自主性有限，存在可靠性问题，护理效果证据主要集中在近端认知参与等方面，建议未来研究转向护理专用评估标准、负责任自主性和临床工作流程整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化导致老年人及患者护理需求快速增长，基础模型被越来越多地集成到机器人和交互式代理中，但其在护理场景中是否能将技术进展转化为临床影响尚不明确，需要系统梳理和评估。

Method: 对基于基础模型的护理机器人进行综合综述，从三个维度（设计特征、用户体验、护理相关结果证据）进行分析和合成。

Result: 当前系统最常将基础模型用作语音为中心的社交辅助机器人中的对话和推理层，多模态基础能力和物理自主性有限；用户体验和可用性评价积极，但可靠性问题（如幻觉、对话中断）持续存在；护理效果证据集中在认知参与等近端结果，缺乏经过验证的临床或护理相关变化证据。

Conclusion: 未来研究应向护理专用的评估标准、可问责的自主性以及与护理工作流程的整合方向转型，以支持更具响应性和责任感的护理技术。

Abstract: Demand for older-adult and patient care is growing rapidly as populations age worldwide. Foundation models are increasingly being integrated into robots and interactive agents, with the promise of more flexible communication and personalized assistance. However, care settings require reliable and workflow-compatible systems with accountable human oversight, and it remains unclear whether current embodied systems can translate technical advances into clinical impact. This Perspective synthesizes foundation model-based care robots across three areas: design features, user experience, and evidence for care-related outcomes. Current systems most commonly use foundation models as conversational and reasoning layers within voice-centered socially assistive embodiments, while multimodal grounding and physical autonomy remain limited. Empirical evaluations report positive usability and engagement benefits, but reliability failures persist across the interaction pipeline such as hallucinations and conversational breakdowns. Evidence for care impact remains concentrated in proximal outcomes such as cognitive engagement and participation, with limited evidence for validated clinical or care-related changes. We argue that future research should transition toward care-specific evaluation standards, accountable autonomy, and integration into care workflows to support more responsive and responsible care technologies.

</details>


### [11] [Multi-UAV Active Sensing with Information Gain-based Planning and Belief Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10986)
*S. Habibi,L. Marques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实地验证了一种基于信息增益的无人机主动感知框架，用于概率性二值地形建模。通过因子图建模空间依赖，比较了IGbIPP与随机游走、扫描覆盖路径规划方法在合成和真实农业影像上的表现，并评估了多种概率融合规则。结果表明，IGbIPP在熵降和地图精度方面优于基线方法，贝叶斯、对数几率与Dempster-Shafer融合效果最佳，等权重或偏置空间权重比自适应权重更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在空间分布环境中的主动感知受限于飞行时间短、感知不确定性以及空间覆盖与观测精度之间的权衡，需要有效的规划与融合方法来提高建图性能。

Method: 采用因子图建模空间依赖关系，基于信息增益的有信息路径规划方法，比较随机游走和扫描覆盖两种基线，在合成地形和真实无人机农业影像上进行验证，并评估不同空间相关权重与多种概率融合规则（贝叶斯、对数几率、Dempster-Shafer等）的效果。

Result: IGbIPP在熵降和建图误差方面显著优于基线方法；宽视场角提升了实际覆盖与地图精度；等权重或偏置空间权重比自适应权重更鲁棒；贝叶斯、对数几率和Dempster-Shafer融合取得了最优的协同建图性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性驱动的路径规划、感知几何、空间建模与概率融合对于实际无人机主动感知至关重要。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for active sensing and information gathering in spatially distributed environments. Their performance, however, is constrained by limited flight time, sensing uncertainty, and the trade-off between spatial coverage and observation accuracy. This paper presents a real-world validation of a multi-UAV active sensing framework for probabilistic binary terrain mapping, with precision agriculture used as the application case. The environment is represented as a probabilistic belief map, where spatial dependencies are modeled through a factor-graph formulation. UAV decision making is guided by Information Gain based Informative Path Planning (IGbIPP), and the approach is compared with Random Walk and Sweep coverage path planning baselines using both synthetic terrains and real UAV-derived agricultural imagery. The study also evaluates spatial correlation weights and several probabilistic belief-fusion rules for multi-UAV information sharing. Results show that IGbIPP reduces entropy and mapping error more effectively than the baselines, while a wider field of view improves real-world coverage and map accuracy. The results further show that simple equal or biased spatial weights can be more robust than adaptive weights, and that Bayesian, log-odds, and Dempster--Shafer fusion achieve the best cooperative mapping performance. These findings highlight the importance of uncertainty-driven planning, sensing geometry, spatial modeling, and probabilistic fusion for real-world UAV-based active sensing.

</details>


### [12] [What Demonstration Curation Metrics Do to Your Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10229)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在机器人操作任务中，用于筛选训练演示的度量标准与基于筛选数据训练的行为克隆策略性能之间的解耦现象。实验表明，缺陷检测能力强的度量标准（AUROC=0.804）产生的策略性能极差（13.3%成功率），而检测能力较弱的度量标准（AUROC=0.638）却能产生接近Oracle的策略（90.0% vs 93.3%）。同时发现7种度量中有5种利用回合长度作为缺陷的代理变量，导致AUROC虚高。研究认为筛选方法应基于其产生的策略性能而非缺陷检测能力来评估，且基准测试必须控制回合长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设能有效检测训练演示中缺陷的度量标准也会提升基于这些筛选数据训练的行为克隆策略的性能。本文旨在检验这一假设是否成立，并揭示两者之间可能存在的解耦现象。

Method: 在LIBERO接触式抓取放置基准任务中引入受控结构缺陷（搬运阶段过早释放夹爪），评估7种不同演示筛选度量标准的缺陷检测AUROC与下游策略成功率之间的关系，并分析回合长度作为混杂变量的影响。

Result: 缺陷检测AUROC最高的度量（0.804）产生的策略成功率最低（13.3%）；AUROC较低的度量（0.638）产生的策略接近Oracle性能（90.0%）。5/7的度量利用回合长度作为缺陷代理变量，控制回合长度后AUROC大幅下降。最优筛选方法可将性能从3.3%提升至接近Oracle的93.3%。

Conclusion: 演示筛选度量应基于其最终产生的策略性能而非缺陷检测能力来评估；任何筛选基准测试必须控制回合长度以避免度量虚高；应直接评估下游策略性能而非仅关注检测准确率。

Abstract: We study whether demonstration-curation metrics that detect defective training episodes also improve the downstream behavior-cloning policy that trains on the curated data. On a contact-rich LIBERO pick-and-place benchmark with a controlled structural defect (early gripper release during the carry phase), we find that the two quantities are sharply decoupled. The metric with the highest defect-detection AUROC (0.804) produces the worst curated policy (13.3% task success), while a metric with a substantially lower AUROC (0.638) produces a policy that nearly matches the oracle trained on ground-truth clean data (90.0% vs. 93.3%). We further show that five of the seven metrics we evaluate exploit episode length as a trivial proxy for the defect label, a confound that inflates reported AUROCs to near-perfect values and disappears once episode length is controlled. Across all conditions, the contaminated baseline succeeds on only 3.3% of rollouts, and the two best curation methods close this to within 3 percentage points of the 93.3% oracle ceiling. Our results argue that curation methods should be evaluated by the policy they produce, not the defects they flag, and that any curation benchmark must control for episode length before reporting detection accuracy. We release the testbed, all metric implementations, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [13] [YUBI: Yielding Universal Bidigital Interface for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10244)
*Takehiko Ohkawa,Jumpei Arima,Yuki Noguchi,Masatoshi Tateno,Makoto Sugiura,Takuya Okubo,Kengo Ikeuchi,Yuma Shin,Hiroki Nishizawa,Naoaki Kanazawa,Yuki Wakayama,Daiki Fukunaga,Koshi Makihara,Tomohiro Motoda,Floris Erich,Yukiyasu Domae,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yohishiro Okumatsu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出YUBI——一种手指对齐的夹爪，通过顺从式手指驱动设计实现直观、符合人体工学的双手灵巧操作数据采集，并构建了包含8434小时、120万 episode、119个任务的超大规模数据集，实验证明YUBI在复杂双手任务中优于UMI夹爪，且单一策略可直接迁移至多种双臂机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手持数据采集系统（如UMI）采用手枪式握把设计，在精细灵巧操作任务中存在人体工学差、可用性低的问题，亟需一种更符合人类手指自然运动的数据采集接口。

Method: 提出YUBI夹爪，核心设计原则为"顺从式手指驱动"（yielding, finger-driven actuation），将人类手指运动直接映射为夹爪开合运动；结合VR 6自由度追踪实现高保真轨迹数据采集；基于此构建大规模数据集。

Result: 收集了8434小时、120万 episode、119个任务的UMI格式数据集；YUBI在复杂双手任务的通用性、灵巧性和操作效率上优于UMI夹爪；单一策略在UR、Franka、ELEY三种双臂机器人平台间直接迁移成功。

Conclusion: YUBI提供了一种可复现的大规模数据采集方案，其硬件、软件和数据集全部开源，为机器人基础模型的发展提供了可扩展的数据获取路径。

Abstract: We introduce Yielding Universal Bidigital Interface (YUBI), a finger-aligned gripper designed to enable intuitive, ergonomic, and scalable data collection for bimanual dexterous manipulation. While handheld data collection systems such as Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) enable affordable data collection, their bulky pistol-grip designs can pose ergonomic and usability challenges for fine-grained, dexterous manipulation tasks. To address this, YUBI presents a distinct design principle: yielding, finger-driven actuation that directly maps human finger movements to gripper jaw motion. Using the YUBI devices, we set up a data collection system with integrated VR-based 6 DoF tracking of the gripper, ensuring high-fidelity trajectory data acquisition. We curate a UMI-based dataset of unprecedented scale: 8,434 hours across 1.20M episodes and 119 tasks. Experiments show that YUBI offers advantages over the UMI gripper in versatility for complex bimanual tasks, dexterity, and operational efficiency. A single policy trained on the YUBI dataset transfers across multiple bimanual robots (UR, Franka, and ELEY) simply by mounting the gripper on each platform, confirming that the collected data are directly executable as policy supervision. We release the gripper hardware, data-collection software, and dataset as one integrated stack, offering the open community a reproducible path to large-scale data acquisition for advancing robotic foundation models.

</details>


### [14] [What Matters in Orchestrating Robot Policies: A Systematic Study of Hierarchical VLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10267)
*Jiaheng Hu,Mohit Shridhar,Caden Lu,Dhruv Shah,Hao-Tien Lewis Chiang,Jie Tan,Annie Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了层次化视觉-语言-动作（Hi-VLA）系统在机器人操作中的设计原则，通过统一框架下的基准测试，提出了构建Hi-VLA系统的实用准则，并证明基于这些原则构建的系统优于平坦VLA控制或简单层次化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hi-VLA系统在设计上缺乏统一原则，不同系统在规划器与控制器选择、两者间切换机制、观测与记忆表示等方面存在差异，亟需系统性的研究来建立设计准则。

Method: 将代表性Hi-VLA智能体统一到options风格的控制框架下，针对短时域、长时域和推理密集型任务基准测试核心设计选择，从中提炼出实用的构建原则。

Result: 模型选择和接口机制共同塑造系统性能；基于提炼原则构建的系统在仿真和真实ALOHA机器人上均显著优于平坦VLA控制或简单层次化设计。

Conclusion: 本工作为构建更强大、鲁棒且具有原则性的层次化VLA智能体提供了基础，验证了系统化设计原则的重要性。

Abstract: Hierarchical vision-language-action (Hi-VLA) systems have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex robot manipulation, by using high-level VLM planners to decompose tasks into language subgoals executed by low-level VLA controllers. Despite recent empirical progress, there is a lack of unified design principles for these systems: existing Hi-VLA systems differ in how they choose and connect planners, controllers, mechanisms to switch between the two, and how observations and memory are represented in the planner. In this paper, we present a systematic study of Hi-VLA design for robot manipulation. We unify representative Hi-VLA agents under an options-style control framework and benchmark core design choices across short-horizon, long-horizon, and reasoning-intensive tasks. Our analysis distills practical principles for building Hi-VLA systems, showing how model choices and interface mechanisms jointly shape performance. Applying these principles yields a substantially stronger system than either flat VLA control or a naively designed hierarchy, across experiments both in simulation and on a real ALOHA robot. Overall, our results provide a foundation for building more capable, robust, and principled hierarchical VLA agents. More information and video at jiahenghu.github.io/hi-vla.

</details>


### [15] [Locomotion analysis of a quadruped interacting with the lunar granular surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10273)
*Yash J Vyas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在模拟月球颗粒状表土（regolith）环境下，使用强化学习训练四足机器人 locomotion 策略。对比了刚体接触与软体接触（模拟月壤）两种环境下的训练结果，发现软接触环境（模拟月壤）会导致训练难度增加、步态发生质变，并显著提高整体能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在月球等外星环境中部署四足机器人面临复杂地形、能量和热约束等挑战。月球表面由颗粒状月壤构成，其对足式机器人 locomotion 性能有显著影响。基于刚体接触假设训练的 locomotion 算法在软接触（如颗粒表面）环境中失效，导致不稳定和跟踪性能差，因此需要研究月壤-机器人足部接触的物理建模及其对强化学习训练的影响。

Method: 将月球颗粒状表面-机器人足部接触的物理模型应用于仿真环境，使用强化学习训练 locomotion 策略。对比训练于刚体接触环境与软接触环境下的策略，分析步态和 locomotion 性能指标。

Result: 软接触（模拟月壤表面）对基于强化学习的训练带来额外挑战，导致生成定性不同的步态，并增加整体能量消耗。

Conclusion: 为有效设计月球探索四足机器人，必须考虑月壤颗粒接触对电机扭矩、能量消耗和运输成本的影响，软接触环境显著改变了强化学习训练出的步态特征和能效表现。

Abstract: Deploying legged robots in extra-terrestrial environments includes many challenges due to complex terrain interactions, energy, and thermal constraints. For effective mechanical design of a lunar exploration quadrupedal robot, careful consideration of motor torques, energy expenditure, and cost of transport is required. The lunar surface is composed of granular regolith, which impacts the locomotion of legged robots and their performance. Locomotion algorithms trained with rigid contact assumptions are also ineffective when applied to environments with soft contacts, such as granular surfaces, which can result in instability and poor tracking. In this report, the physical modelling of the granular lunar surface-robot foot contacts is applied to a simulation environment with locomotion trained using Reinforcement Learning. A comparison is conducted between the policy trained on rigid contact and soft contact environments, analysing the gait and locomotion performance metrics. The analysis demonstrates that soft contacts simulating regolith surfaces pose additional challenges for Reinforcement Learning based training, result in a qualitatively different gait, and increase the overall energy expenditure.

</details>


### [16] [Hierarchical Policies from Verbal and Egocentric Human Signals for Natural Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10276)
*Dongjun Lee,Juheon Choi,Dong Kyu Shin,Sinjae Kang,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EDITH是一个机器人框架，通过智能眼镜捕捉人类的第一人称视角、注视和手势等非语言信号，结合语言指令作为机器人策略的输入。系统采用分层策略：高层策略从多模态信号中推断人类意图并生成子任务序列（每个子任务包含细粒度指令和关键帧），低层策略执行这些子任务。实验表明，EDITH使机器人能理解短暂表达的非语言意图，并显著减少用户传达意图所需的工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略仅依赖语言指令作为传达意图的唯一接口，忽略了手势和注视等非语言信号，将所有沟通负担都放在语言上，导致交互效率低下且不自然。

Method: 硬件系统通过智能眼镜实时流式传输人类的第一人称视角、注视和语音，并将语音转录为语言指令。设计分层策略：高层策略从多模态信号推断意图并生成子任务序列（每个子任务由细粒度指令和场景关键帧表示），低层策略执行子任务。

Result: EDITH使机器人能够理解仅通过简短非语言信号表达的意图，与仅使用语言指令相比，显著减少了用户传达意图所需的工作量。

Conclusion: 结合非语言信号（如注视和手势）与语言指令的多模态交互框架能有效提升人机交互的自然性和效率，减轻用户的沟通负担。

Abstract: For natural human-robot interaction, a robot must understand human intent expressed not only through language but also through nonverbal signals such as gestures and gaze. However, current robot policies rely on language instructions as the sole interface for conveying intent, leaving nonverbal signals unused and placing the full burden of communication. In this work, we present EDITH, a robot framework that captures the human's nonverbal signals through continuous streams of first-person view and gaze from smart glasses, and uses them alongside language instructions as inputs to the robot policy. Our hardware system streams the human's first-person view, gaze, and speech to the robot in real time, transcribing the speech into language instructions. To handle these rich but noisy signals, we design a hierarchical policy in which a high-level policy infers the human's intent and produces a sequence of subtasks, where each subtask is represented as a fine-grained instruction paired with a keyframe that grounds the intent in the scene (e.g., the frame where the human points at the target object). A low-level policy then executes these subtasks. In our experiments on human-robot interactive tasks, EDITH enables the robot to act on the human's nonverbal signals even when intent is expressed only briefly, and significantly reduces user effort to convey intent compared to using language instructions alone. Visit our project page for source code and real-robot demo videos.

</details>


### [17] [MARCH: Model-Assisted Reinforcement Learning for the Perceptive Control of Humanoids over Sparse Footholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10288)
*Codrin Crismariu,Ryan K. Cosner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模型辅助强化学习框架，结合简化模型生成安全参考轨迹、控制李雅普诺夫函数引导教师策略训练、以及视觉学生策略蒸馏，实现了稀疏地形上的双足 locomotion，在仿真和 Unitree G1 机器人上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏地形上的双足 locomotion 仍具挑战：基于模型的方法精确但对不确定性脆弱，无模型方法鲁棒但难以发现安全关键运动所需的精确约束动作。

Method: 三步框架：(1) 用简化模型生成安全参考轨迹；(2) 基于参考轨迹构建控制李雅普诺夫函数（CLF）奖励，训练特权教师策略；(3) 将教师策略蒸馏为基于视觉的学生策略。

Result: 提高了样本效率，减少了对复杂学习课程的需求，实现了更平滑的运动行为，在 stepping stone 任务上的表现与无模型基线相当，并在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上成功部署。

Conclusion: 模型辅助的 RL 框架能够有效结合基于模型和无模型方法的优势，在稀疏地形上产生物理合理的双足运动。

Abstract: Perceptive bipedal locomotion over sparse terrain remains a difficult challenge: model-based methods are precise but brittle to uncertainty, while model-free methods are robust but struggle to discover the precise, constrained motions required for safety-critical locomotion where small errors can cause catastrophic failures. We propose a model-assisted reinforcement learning (RL) framework that combines both perspectives in three steps: (1) generate a safe reference trajectory using simplified models; (2) train a privileged teacher policy guided by a control Lyapunov function (CLF) reward built around the safe reference trajectory; and (3) distill the teacher into a vision-based student policy. We show that this model-assistance procedure produces physically grounded locomotion, improving sample efficiency, reducing the need for a complex learning curriculum, and achieving smoother locomotion behavior alongside stepping stone performance comparable to model-free baselines. We validate our approach in simulation and demonstrate successful deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot navigating sparse footholds with lateral constraints.

</details>


### [18] [Improved Representation of Matrix Lie Group Operations through Tensor Notation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10289)
*Clark Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将张量和爱因斯坦求和符号应用于矩阵李群的操作与导数计算，旨在提供更清晰、简洁的数学符号，简化基于梯度的估计框架中矩阵李群的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的研究表明，在估计问题中使用李群可以提高精度和一致性。然而，现有的数学表示在处理矩阵李群的导数和运算时较为复杂，缺乏直观性。因此，需要一种更清晰的符号系统来简化相关工作。

Method: 引入张量和爱因斯坦求和符号来表述矩阵李群的运算和矩阵李导数，将这一已广泛应用于其他领域的数学工具适配到矩阵李群的表示与分析中。

Result: 新的符号系统能够更清晰地表达矩阵李群所需的导数和运算，使得在基于梯度的估计框架中操作矩阵李群更加直观和便捷。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献并非提出新的功能，而是提供了一种更清晰、明了的数学表示方法，有助于推动矩阵李群在估计问题中的进一步应用。

Abstract: Several recent papers have demonstrated the utility of using Lie groups within estimation problems, yielding improved accuracy and consistency. This paper introduces a new tool for describing operations with matrix Lie groups: tensors and the Einstein summation notation. While tensors and Einstein notation are well-known in other research fields, applying this mathematical notation to represent and compute matrix Lie derivatives is novel. More importantly, this new notation greatly clarifies the derivatives and operations necessary to work with matrix Lie Groups in (gradient-based) estimation frameworks. Therefore, the main contribution of this paper is not a new capability, but a more perspicuous mathematical notation for working with matrix Lie groups.

</details>


### [19] [SARM2: Multi-Task Stage Aware Reward Modeling for Self Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10305)
*Qianzhong Chen,Hau Zheng,Justin Yu,Suning Huang,Jiankai Sun,Ken Goldberg,Chuan Wen,Pieter Abbeel,Yide Shentu,Philipp Wu,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务阶段感知奖励模型RM，结合动作基元阶段估计器和多门专家混合（MMoE）价值头，为长时程操作任务生成密集逐步骤奖励；并在此基础上提出SPIRAL框架，利用廉价自主轨迹进行策略优化，在10个任务上将成功率从约50%提升至接近完美（如折叠短裤58%→100%，清洁白板50%→90%），价值估计MSE降低80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略微调严重依赖行为克隆，需要昂贵的高质量演示且策略局限于演示分布；而奖励模型虽可通过强化学习降低这种依赖，但现有方法要么需要逐任务标注（stage-aware模型），要么粒度太粗无法捕捉长时程细粒度进展（通用VLM奖励模型），缺乏既准确又通用的密集奖励信号。

Method: 1) 提出RM多任务阶段感知奖励模型，包含基于动作基元的阶段估计器来识别任务进度，以及多门专家混合（MMoE）价值头来生成密集逐步骤奖励。2) 基于RM提出SPIRAL（Self-Policy Improvement via Reward-Aligned Learning）框架，利用策略自主滚动的廉价数据，通过奖励引导进行在线策略优化。

Result: 在10个任务的基准上，RM的价值估计MSE相比最强基线降低80%；在SPIRAL框架下，Folding Shorts任务成功率从58%提升至100%，Cleaning Whiteboard从50%提升至90%，整体从约50%提升至接近完美。

Conclusion: 高质量的密集奖励信号是实现稳定机器人数据飞轮（robot data flywheel）的关键，表明基于动作基元阶段估计与多专家混合架构的奖励模型能够有效弥补现有方法的准确性与通用性 gap，显著提升长时程操作任务中VLA策略的性能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language-action (VLA) policies for long-horizon manipulation still relies heavily on behavior cloning, which requires costly high-quality demonstrations and keeps policies near the demonstration distribution. Reward models can reduce this dependence by reweighting demonstrations and providing dense supervision for on-robot reinforcement learning (RL), but they must be dense, accurate, and general. Existing methods fall short: task-specific stage-aware models are accurate but require per-task annotations, while general vision-language-model (VLM) reward models are broadly applicable but too coarse for fine-grained long-horizon progress. We introduce RM, a multi-task stage-aware reward model that combines an action-primitive-based stage estimator with a multi-gate Mixture-of-Experts (MMoE) value head to produce dense per-step rewards across manipulation tasks. Building on RM, we further propose SPIRAL (Self-Policy Improvement via Reward-Aligned Learning), an on-policy reward-guided framework that improves VLA policies from cheap autonomous rollouts. On a 10-task benchmark, RM reduces value-estimation MSE by 80% over the strongest baselines; when used in SPIRAL, it improves task success from around 50% to near-perfect performance on Folding Shorts (58% to 100%) and Cleaning Whiteboard (50% to 90%), showing that high-quality dense rewards are key to a stable robot data flywheel. Project website: https://qianzhong-chen.github.io/sarm2.github.io/.

</details>


### [20] [OMG: Omni-Modal Motion Generation for Generalist Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10340)
*Siqiao Huang,Kun-Ying Lee,Dongming Qiao,Guanqi He,Zhenyu Wang,Yitang Li,Shaoting Zhu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMG提出了一种面向人形机器人的全模态全身控制框架，通过分层架构（可扩展大脑+反应性小脑）、精细的数据流水线和扩散运动生成模型，实现了对语言、音频和参考动作等多种模态的通用控制，展现出SOTA性能和良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形全身控制方法要么局限于少量技能策略且依赖繁重的奖励工程，要么难以扩展到新的输入模态。关键在于构建一个可扩展的"大脑"模块，使其具备处理多样化条件模态的推理能力，从而迈向通用人形机器人控制。

Method: 提出OMG框架，采用分层结构（可扩展大脑+反应性运动追踪小脑），通过精细的数据整理、过滤和标注流水线获取高质量数据，并基于扩散模型构建运动生成主干，支持语言、音频和人体参考动作等多种条件输入。

Result: 在多种模态条件下展现出SOTA性能，具备良好的模型扩展行为，能高效适应新数据分布和新模态。

Conclusion: OMG是一个全模态全身控制器，为迈向人形机器人基础模型迈出了坚实的一步。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body control has made significant progress in recent years, yet existing approaches remain limited to few-skill policies with heavy reward engineering, or motion trackers that are difficult to extend to new input modalities. We argue that the key to general-purpose humanoid control is to build a scalable brain, a module capable of reasoning with diverse conditioning modalities, atop a reactive motion tracking cerebellum, mirroring the hierarchical structure of biological motor systems. Two challenges arise in realizing this vision: acquiring a vast amount of high-quality data to achieve general purpose control, and equipping the generator with the capability to condition on compositional, extensible multi-modal inputs. We present OMG, which addresses these challenges with a meticulous data curation, filtering and labeling pipeline, as well as a diffusion-based motion generation backbone that conditions on language, audio, and human reference motions. Extensive experiments validate OMG as an omni-modal whole-body controller exhibiting state-of-the-art performance, model scaling behavior and efficient adaptation to new distributions and modalities, marking a concrete step toward foundation models for humanoid robots.

</details>


### [21] [Rethinking Embodied Navigation via Relational Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10348)
*Weitao An,Chenghao Xu,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DB-Nav通过双重关系偏置（激活偏置和抑制偏置）重塑搜索空间，利用在线观测和失败访问信息调节前沿探索价值，在物体导航任务中显著提升了成功率和路径效率，且无需昂贵的在线VLM推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体导航方法依赖开放词汇检测器或VLM来回答"去哪里搜索"，但忽略了"什么不可信"——即哪些语义线索是不可靠的。开放词汇感知容易产生系统性的误导证据（假阳性、过时的静态先验、缺乏具身体验证导致的重复失败探索），这些错误根植于真实场景中的结构化物体关系，污染了地图构建和决策过程。

Method: 提出DB-Nav框架，将目标中心关系分解为激活偏置（传播上下文证据）和抑制偏置（通过感知混淆和行为级证伪抑制不可靠区域），统一构建关系激活-抑制探索图，利用在线观测和失败访问信息调制前沿探索值。

Result: 在ObjectNav基准测试上，DB-Nav在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DB-Nav提供了一个轻量级、可解释且鲁棒的导航框架，无需昂贵的在线VLM推理即可有效处理物体导航中语义线索不可靠的问题。

Abstract: Object navigation requires an agent to locate a target in an unknown environment through visual observations. Existing methods typically rely on open-vocabulary detectors or vision-language models (VLMs) to answer where to search, but often overlook what not to trust - which semantic cues are unreliable. Open-vocabulary perception is prone to systematic misleading evidence: false positives, outdated static priors, and repeated failed exploration due to lack of embodied verification, which contaminates mapping and decision-making. Such errors are rooted in structured object relations in real-world scenes. To address this, we propose DB-Nav, a framework that reshapes the search space via dual relational biases. It factorizes target-centric relations into an Activation Bias (propagates contextual evidence) and an Inhibition Bias (suppresses unreliable regions via perceptual confusion and action-level falsification). These biases are unified into a Relational Activation-Inhibition Exploration Graph that modulates frontier exploration values using online observations and failed accesses. Experiments on ObjectNav benchmarks show that DB-Nav significantly outperforms existing methods in success rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), offering a lightweight, interpretable, and robust navigation framework without costly online VLM reasoning.

</details>


### [22] [HiMem-WAM: Hierarchical Memory-Gated World Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10363)
*Xiaoquan Sun,Ruijian Zhang,Chen Cao,Yihan Sun,Jiahui Chen,Zetian Xu,Bo Chen,Haijier Chen,Zhen Yang,Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,JingZhe Xu,Jingrui Pang,Mingqi Yuan,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiMem-WAM 提出了一种层次化记忆门控的世界动作模型，通过运动/技能潜变量分层框架和边界感知记忆更新机制，提升了长程机器人操作任务中的任务相关记忆能力，在多个基准和真实任务中显著增强了鲁棒性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）在长程机器人操作中仍难以有效利用任务相关的记忆信息，导致在持续性操作任务中性能下降。

Method: 提出 HiMem-WAM，包含三个核心组件：(1) 层次化潜变量动作框架，联合学习低层运动潜变量和高层技能潜变量，实现结构化时间抽象；(2) 边界感知记忆门，在预测的技能转换边界处写入紧凑任务状态，实现因果推理；(3) 无需在测试时生成未来视频或估计光流。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、RMBench 以及真实世界任务上的评估表明，层次化潜变量提升了部署扰动下的鲁棒性，记忆模块显著促进了依赖记忆的长程操作任务。

Conclusion: HiMem-WAM 通过层次化记忆机制有效解决了 WAMs 在长程操作中的记忆缺失问题，在多个基准和真实场景中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a new powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, learning action-relevant visual dynamics that significantly enhance generalization and robustness. However, existing WAMs still struggle with task-relevant memory in long-horizon robotic manipulation. To address this, we present HiMem-WAM, a Hierarchical Memory-Gated WAM that integrates motion-centric latent actions, high-level skill latents, and boundary-triggered memory updates. Specifically, we develop a hierarchical latent action framework that jointly learns low-level motion and high-level skill latents, providing structured temporal abstraction. Meanwhile, a boundary-aware memory gate writes compact task states at predicted skill transitions, enabling causal inference without test-time generation of future video or optical flow estimation. Evaluated on LIBERO, LIBERO-PLUS, RMBench and real-world tasks, HiMem-WAM shows that hierarchical latents improve robustness under deployment perturbations, and the memory module substantially benefits memory-dependent long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [23] [A Practical Recipe Towards Improving Sim-and-Real Correlation for VLA Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10366)
*Shuo Wang,Hanyuan Xu,Yingdong Hu,Fanqi Lin,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中仿真与真实环境之间的相关性，提出了量化仿真评估可靠性的统一框架，并分析了仿真用于策略改进的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管仿真基准在真实感和多样性方面取得了显著进展，但尚未被广泛接受为可靠的真实策略评估代理。本文旨在系统探究仿真与真实评估之间的相关性，揭示现有仿真平台的局限性。

Method: 跨多个仿真平台、VLA策略、任务和扰动因素，系统测量仿真评估在策略排名一致性、性能相关性和扰动故障模式方面能否保持真实世界的结论。

Result: 识别出现有仿真器的局限性，明确哪些仿真信号更贴近真实部署；发现仿真微调在何种条件下有益，以及后训练数据量如何影响仿真-真实对齐。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统一的框架用于衡量、解释和提升仿真对VLA策略的有用性，为仿真平台设计者和利用仿真进行策略开发的实践者提供指导。

Abstract: Simulation has become an essential tool for evaluating and improving vision-language-action (VLA) policies, offering scalable, reproducible, and controllable alternatives to costly real-world robot evaluation. Recent simulation benchmarks have made substantial progress on realism and diversity, yet these platforms have not been widely adopted as reliable proxies for real-world policy evaluation. In this work, we investigate this issue through the lens of sim-and-real correlation. We conduct a systematic study across multiple simulation platforms, VLA policies, tasks, and perturbation factors, measuring whether simulated evaluation preserves real-world conclusions in terms of policy ranking consistency, performance correlation, and perturbation-wise failure patterns. This analysis allows us to characterize the limitations of existing simulators and identify what kinds of simulation signals are more aligned with real-world deployment. We further examine how users should exploit simulation for policy improvement, including when simulator-based finetuning is beneficial and how the amount of post-training data affects sim-and-real alignment. Overall, our work provides a unified framework for measuring, interpreting, and improving the usefulness of simulation for VLA policies, offering guidance both for simulator designers and for practitioners who use simulation as part of the policy development pipeline.

</details>


### [24] [Test-time Adversarial Takeover: A Real-time Hijacking Interface against Robotic Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10371)
*Zi Yin,Peilin Chai,Siyuan Huang,Zhanhao Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时对抗接管（TAKO）攻击，通过可微扩散推理学习少量可复用通用补丁，在测试时切换这些视觉补丁即可将冻结的扩散策略转变为远程操控工具，在多种任务、编码器和推理方法上实现100%接管成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对扩散策略的攻击主要关注破坏（降低任务成功率或引发混乱行为），而作者研究更强的威胁：攻击者能否实时接管冻结的机器人策略，将其变为远程操控工具。

Method: TAKO通过可微扩散推理学习小词表（少量可复用的通用补丁），测试时在相机流中切换这些补丁以组合出攻击者指定的轨迹。扰动作用于视觉条件路径，诱导偏差可在迭代生成推理中持续存在。

Result: 在4类任务（2D操作、模拟空中配送、模拟地面导航、物理世界地面导航）、2种视觉编码器（ResNet-18和EfficientNet-B0+Transformer）、3种生成推理族（DDPM、DDIM、流匹配）中，人类操作员在所有评估设置下均实现100%接管成功率。

Conclusion: 自然的目标匹配基线（target-policy matching）因受害策略无法可靠地在分布外目标偏移上自我监督而失败，而TAKO成功实现了对扩散策略的完全接管，揭示了严重安全漏洞。

Abstract: Diffusion-based action generation has become a foundational component of embodied AI, but its reliance on visual conditioning leaves deployed visuomotor policies vulnerable to adversarial manipulation. Most prior attacks focus on disruption: they perturb the observation stream to reduce task success or induce erratic behavior. We study a stronger threat, Test-time Adversarial Takeover (TAKO), in which an attacker obtains a real-time steering interface over a frozen robot policy and turns it into a remotely piloted instrument. TAKO learns a small vocabulary of reusable universal patches through differentiable diffusion inference; at test time, the attacker switches among these patches in the camera stream to compose attacker-chosen trajectories. This works because the perturbation acts on the visual conditioning pathway, where the induced bias can persist through iterative generative inference. We further show that the natural targeted baseline, target-policy matching, fails because the victim policy cannot reliably supervise itself on out-of-distribution target shifts. Across four tasks (2D manipulation, simulated aerial delivery, simulated ground navigation, and physical-world ground navigation), two visual encoders (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0 + Transformer), and three generative inference families (DDPM, DDIM, and flow matching), human operators achieve 100\% takeover success on attacker-defined objectives in every evaluated setting. The project page is available at https://tako-attack.github.io.

</details>


### [25] [UMI-Bench 1.0: An Open and Reproducible Real-World Benchmark for Tabletop Robotic Manipulation with UMI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10382)
*Shi Jin,Yuntian Wang,Yuhui Duan,Di Wu,Gaoqi Dong,Xiaohang Liu,Xiaotong Li,Hongfei Jia,Zehao Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhongjie Jia,Yuanqi Yao,Chenjia Bai,Zhaxizhuoma,Siao Liu,Nieqing Cao,Jin Wang,Chao Yu,Yan Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-Bench 1.0 是首个专门用于标准化评估UMI风格操作策略的真实机器人基准测试平台，统一了数据收集、场景重置、策略执行、结果记录和任务因素分析的全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人评估对于理解学习到的操作策略能否在精心策划的演示之外可靠运行至关重要，尤其是UMI风格策略的性能高度依赖于腕部视角、动作表示、数据收集和物理部署之间的耦合，而现有基准测试并非针对这种数据到部署的设置设计。

Method: 提出了UMI-Bench 1.0，一个本地优先的真实机器人基准测试平台，通过统一的数据收集、场景重置、策略执行、结果记录和任务因素分析协议，实现标准化评估。

Result: 这是首个专门用于真实世界评估UMI操作模型的基准测试，使完整的评估过程可复现和可审计。

Conclusion: UMI-Bench为测量UMI训练策略在真实物理操作中的泛化能力提供了一个实用的测试平台。

Abstract: Real-robot evaluation is essential for understanding whether learned manipulation policies can operate reliably outside curated demonstrations. This need is particularly pressing for Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI)-style policies, whose performance depends on the coupling between wrist-view observations, action representation, data collection, and physical deployment. Existing real-world benchmarks have made important progress, but they are not designed around this UMI data-to-deployment setting. We present UMI-Bench 1.0, a local-first real-robot benchmark for standardized evaluation of UMI-style manipulation policies. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark dedicated to real-world evaluation of UMI-based manipulation models. UMI-Bench aligns data collection, scene reset, policy execution, result logging, and task-factor analysis within a unified protocol. By making the full evaluation process reproducible and auditable, UMI-Bench provides a practical testbed for measuring how UMI-trained policies generalize to real physical manipulation.

</details>


### [26] [Information-Preserving Continuous Occupancy Mapping with Variance-Weighted Submap Joining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10442)
*Zhuhua Bai,Yingyu Wang,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个连续概率子图拼接框架，利用稀疏贝叶斯公式在隐对数几率空间联合优化子图位姿与全局占据场，相比离散网格方法实现更高精度与更优全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据栅格子图拼接方法在离散网格上操作，导致优化梯度不光滑且忽略占据估计的不确定性，亟需一种连续概率框架来克服这些局限。

Method: 采用信息保持的稀疏贝叶斯公式，将原始占据观测压缩为充分统计量对数几率元组，保留后验信息；推导闭合形式的预测均值和方差，构建具有解析雅可比矩阵的子图拼接目标函数，位姿收敛后获得闭合形式的最优全局地图。

Result: 在仿真和大规模真实数据集上，所提方法相比最先进的网格基线实现了更高位姿精度和全局一致性，地图表示更紧凑，不确定性估计比现有连续占据建图方法更优。

Conclusion: 连续概率子图拼接框架有效解决了离散方法的梯度与不确定性问题，为大规模SLAM提供了一种兼顾精度、紧凑性与不确定性校准的新范式。

Abstract: Large-scale SLAM remains challenging due to accumulated trajectory drift and the increasing computational cost of maintaining global consistency. Submap joining alleviates these issues by constructing locally consistent submaps and subsequently fusing them into a global map. However, existing occupancy-based submap joining methods operate on discrete grids, resulting in non-smooth gradients during optimization and neglecting the uncertainty associated with occupancy estimates. We propose the first continuous probabilistic submap joining framework that jointly optimizes submap poses and a global occupancy field in the latent log-odds space. The framework employs an information-preserving sparse Bayesian formulation that compresses raw occupancy observations into sufficient-statistic log-odds tuples while retaining the posterior information of the original observations. This yields closed-form predictive mean and variance estimates for occupancy mapping, which directly enable a submap joining formulation with analytical Jacobians, leading to more accurate submap joining and yielding a closed-form optimal global map upon pose convergence. Experiments on both simulated and large-scale real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher pose accuracy and improved global consistency than state-of-the-art grid-based submap joining approaches, while producing more compact map representations and better-calibrated uncertainty estimates than existing continuous occupancy mapping methods.

</details>


### [27] [GuideWalk: Learning Unified Autonomous Navigation and Locomotion for Humanoid Robots across Versatile Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10449)
*Haoxuan Han,Chen Chen,Linao Gong,Xin Yang,Hao Hu,Junhong Guo,Zhicheng He,Yao Su,Fenghua He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuideWalk是一个统一的端到端框架，将可通行性感知的导航引导与地形自适应 locomotion 教师策略相结合，实现人形机器人在多样化地形上的稳定导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人虽已具备强大的运动能力，但在复杂地形上的可靠导航仍具挑战性，因为避障必须与动态可行的运动协调一致。

Method: 提出导航模块提供显式速度引导解耦避障与地形条件；提出复合教师蒸馏方案聚合目标导向指令与动态一致动作并蒸馏为单一策略；通过强化学习和辅助行为克隆目标对蒸馏策略进行精炼。

Result: GuideWalk在保持稳定人形运动的同时实现了稳定有效的导航。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了人形机器人在多样化地形上的导航问题，实现了避障与动态运动协调的统一端到端方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved strong locomotion capabilities, but reliable navigation on versatile terrains remains challenging because obstacle avoidance must be coordinated with dynamically feasible motion. In this work, we present GuideWalk, a unified end-to-end framework that integrates traversability-aware navigation guidance with terrain-adaptive locomotion teacher for humanoid navigation. Specifically, we introduce a navigation module that provides explicit velocity guidance, decoupling obstacle avoidance from terrain conditions to enable robust planning across diverse environments. We propose a composite teacher distillation scheme, where goal-directed commands and dynamically consistent actions are aggregated and distilled into a single policy. To further improve robustness, the distilled policy is refined with reinforcement learning and an auxiliary behavior cloning objective, which promotes exploration while preserving desirable teacher behaviors. Experiments demonstrate that GuideWalk achieves stable and effective navigation while maintaining stable humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [28] [Act on What You See: Unlocking Safe Social Navigation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10495)
*Qingzi Wang,Xiyang Wu,Guangyao Shi,Dianwei Chen,Xianfeng Yang,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SALSA框架，通过两阶段无注释后训练（社会行为对齐+时间安全对齐），让预训练VLA模型利用其已有内部表征实现更安全的社交导航，将近碰撞率降低86.4%，反事实准确率从53%提升至93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的VLA模型内部编码了行人-物体区分和未来碰撞信号，但行为克隆无法将这些信号转化为社交上合理的动作，导致机器人无法在社交导航中做出安全反应。

Method: 两阶段无注释后训练框架SALSA：(1) 社会行为对齐——桥接中间层社交特征到动作头，并在反事实人-物场景对上训练以打破视觉显著性捷径；(2) 时间安全对齐——提供自动生成的未来风险监督，实现预期性碰撞避免。

Result: 在SCAND和真实部署中，近碰撞减少86.4%，社交反事实准确率从53%提升至93%。

Conclusion: 通过更好地对齐VLA策略的潜表征与动作生成，可以将预训练的VLA策略适配为更安全的社交导航策略。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires robots to distinguish people from ordinary obstacles and to react before danger becomes imminent. We show that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models already encode pedestrian-object distinctions and future collision signals in their internal representations, but behavior cloning fails to translate these signals into socially appropriate actions. To address this mismatch, we propose SALSA, a two-stage annotation-free post-training framework: (1) social behavioral alignment bridges intermediate-layer social features to the action head and trains on counterfactual human-object scene pairs to break visual saliency shortcuts; (2) temporal safety alignment provides automatically generated future-risk supervision to enable anticipatory collision avoidance. On SCAND and real-world deployment, SALSA reduces near-collisions by 86.4% and improves social counterfactual accuracy from 53% to 93%, demonstrating that safer social navigation can be achieved by teaching VLA policies to act on representations they already possess. These results show that pretrained VLA policies can be adapted for safer social navigation by better aligning their latent representations with action generation.

</details>


### [29] [Uncovering Vulnerability of Vision-Language-Action Models under Joint-Level Physical Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10501)
*Minsoo Jo,Taeju Kwon,Junha Chun,Youngjoon Jeong,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA模型在机器人关节级物理故障下的脆弱性，并提出轻量级残差校准框架J-PARC，通过推断潜在故障模式并自适应校正动作，提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人常因执行器退化、硬件故障、安全限制、碰撞损伤或磨损摩擦等关节级物理故障导致动作-运动接口改变，破坏策略的闭环关系，而现有VLA模型对此类故障的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出J-PARC（关节级物理故障感知残差校准器），基于冻结的VLA策略，从近期关节动力学中推断潜在故障模式，并以此条件化共享残差校准器，实现跨故障关节的自适应动作校正。

Result: VLA模型对关节级故障敏感，不同关节故障影响异质；性能下降不能仅归因于物理不可行性（如摩擦增加仍显著降低成功率）；J-PARC在故障环境下提升鲁棒性同时保持无故障环境性能。

Conclusion: 关节级物理故障严重威胁VLA策略的闭环执行，J-PARC通过轻量级残差校准有效缓解此问题，为机器人系统部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in real robotic systems requires robustness not only to semantic and perceptual variations, but also to embodiment-side faults that change how actions are physically realized. Real robots can experience joint-level changes caused by actuator degradation, hardware faults, safety limits, collision damage, or wear-induced friction. These faults are critical because they alter the action-to-motion interface of a policy, disrupting the learned closed-loop relationship between commanded actions, realized motion, and subsequent observations. In this work, we study realistic joint-level physical faults and show that VLA models are vulnerable when predicted actions are executed through a perturbed robot body. Our analysis reveals joint-dependent effects, with heterogeneous degradation in task success across affected joints. We also show that performance drops cannot be attributed solely to physical infeasibility, since feasible faults such as increased joint friction can still substantially reduce success rates and induce closed-loop execution mismatch. Motivated by these findings, we propose Joint-level Physical-fault Aware Residual Calibrator (J-PARC), a lightweight residual calibration framework built on top of a frozen VLA policy. J-PARC infers a latent joint-fault regime from recent joint dynamics and conditions a shared residual calibrator on this regime, enabling adaptive action correction across faulty joints. Experiments show that J-PARC improves robustness under joint-level faults while preserving fault-free environment performance.

</details>


### [30] [VeriSpace: Spatially Grounded Action Verification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10568)
*Guiyu Zhao,Longteng Guo,Junyou Zhu,Jun Fu,Yanghong Mei,Bin Cao,Jie Jiang,Xingjian He,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VeriSpace，一种3D感知的动作验证器，用于VLA系统的测试时动作选择，通过双路径3D注入场景编码和空间接地动作推理来评估候选动作的几何有效性和任务进度，显著提升操作可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中表现强劲，但测试时可靠性受限于单次动作预测，微小动作错误会导致抓取失败、碰撞或任务错误推进。因此需要测试时验证机制来评估多个候选动作。

Method: VeriSpace包含两个核心组件：(1) 双路径3D注入场景编码，构建同时保留视觉语义和显式3D几何的场景表示；(2) 空间接地动作推理，通过推理任务相关的空间关系、几何有效性和预期目标进度来评估每个动作。

Result: 在公开基准和真实机器人操作任务上，VeriSpace持续提升了底层VLA策略和先前基于验证的方法的决策可靠性，在分布内和分布外场景中均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: VeriSpace能够更可靠地区分细微但结果关键的动作候选，同时完全兼容现有VLA策略，为测试时动作选择提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for robotic manipulation, but their reliability at test time remains limited by one-shot action prediction, where even small action errors can cause grasp failure, collision, or incorrect task progression. A natural alternative is to equip VLA systems with test-time verification, allowing multiple candidate actions to be proposed and evaluated before execution. However, reliable action verification is challenging because it requires not only distinguishing subtle geometric differences between candidate actions, but also assessing whether an action makes meaningful progress toward the task goal. We present VeriSpace, a 3D-aware action verifier for test-time action selection in VLA systems. VeriSpace evaluates candidate actions through two key components: Dual-Path 3D-Injected Scene Encoding, which constructs a scene representation that jointly preserves visual semantics and explicit 3D geometry, and Spatially-Grounded Action Reasoning, which evaluates each action by reasoning over task-relevant spatial relations, geometric validity, and expected goal progress. Together, these components enable more reliable discrimination between subtle yet outcome-critical action candidates while remaining fully compatible with existing VLA policies. Experiments on public benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks show that VeriSpace consistently improves decision reliability over both underlying VLA policies and prior verification-based methods, yielding substantial gains in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [31] [AgenticNav: Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation as a Tool-Calling Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10577)
*Yijian Li,Changze Li,Hantian Shi,Jiaying Luo,Jiyuan Cai,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticNav，一种零样本VLN-CE的轻量级导航框架，通过将动作、深度和记忆作为可调用工具暴露给VLM，在R2R-CE基准上取得零样本方法的最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本VLN-CE方法依赖学习的航点预测器，限制了动作空间且未能有效利用深度输入；同时记忆管理通过积累冗长的文本/视觉历史或检索跨episode经验，削弱了零样本设定。

Method: AgenticNav将VLM与环境之间的交互设计为智能体接口，包含三个工具：(1) 动作工具：允许VLM直接选择RGB观测中的目标像素并转换为可执行动作；(2) 深度工具：按需提供像素级精确度量距离；(3) 记忆工具：紧凑地图图像总结历史轨迹，配合召回工具让VLM选择性回顾过去视觉观察。

Result: 在R2R-CE基准上，使用相同VLM骨干网络时取得零样本方法SOTA；真实世界实验验证了零样本泛化能力；消融实验表明动作工具设计优于传统航点预测器，深度工具和智能体记忆进一步提升了导航性能。

Conclusion: AgenticNav通过将关键导航能力工具化，有效克服了现有零样本VLN-CE方法的局限性，为VLM驱动的连续环境导航提供了更灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) has recently become feasible with large vision-language models (VLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on learned waypoint predictors to propose navigable actions. This severely limits the model's action space and fails to leverage depth inputs effectively. Moreover, memory is commonly handled by accumulating long textual or visual histories with substantial irrelevant context, or by retrieving cross-episode experiences, which weakens the zero-shot setting. In this paper, we rethink zero-shot VLN-CE as an agentic interface between the VLM and the environment, and present AgenticNav, a lightweight navigation harness that exposes action, depth, and memory as callable tools. Instead of choosing from predicted waypoints, the action tool allows the VLM to directly select a target pixel in RGB observations, converting it into executable motion. Depth is exposed through an on-demand pixel-depth tool, enabling the VLM to request precise metric distances only where they matter. For memory, AgenticNav provides a compact map image summarizing the historical trajectory, paired with a recall tool that allows the VLM to selectively revisit past visual observations without overwhelming the prompt context. On the R2R-CE benchmark, AgenticNav establishes new state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among zero-shot methods given the same VLM backbone. Real-world validation further highlights its zero-shot generalization compared to prior methods. Ablations show that our action tool design outperforms traditional waypoint predictors, and that depth tool and agentic memory further contribute to navigation performance.

</details>


### [32] [Dexterous Point Policy: Learning Point-based Dexterous Hand Policies from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10614)
*Beomjun Kim,Seong Hyeon Park,Seunghoon Sim,Seungjun Moon,Sanghyeok Lee,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dexterous Point Policy框架，通过统一的3D关键点表示直接从人类视频学习灵巧操作策略，无需机器人演示，在真实机器人任务上达到75%成功率，而VLA基线仅1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型在部署时存在明显的具身差距（embodiment gap），微调需要大量昂贵的机器人数据，尤其在灵巧操作领域数据收集极为耗时。

Method: 从人类视频中提取任务相关物体和人手的3D关键点（手腕和指尖），训练自回归Transformer模型，利用关键点层次上人类与机器人行为的对齐实现策略直接迁移。

Result: 在真实机器人拾放和工具使用任务上达到75.0%成功率，而VLA基线仅1.0%；在包含多物体环境和新物体类别的未见场景中具有较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 统一的3D关键点表示能够有效弥合人类与机器人之间的具身差距，实现无需机器人演示的灵巧操作策略学习。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models pre-trained on human demonstration videos have shown promise, but a significant embodiment gap remains when the resulting policies are deployed on real robots. A common remedy is to fine-tune these models on robot-specific demonstrations. However, robot data collection can be prohibitively expensive and time-consuming, which is particularly acute in dexterous manipulation, e.g., teleoperating a multi-fingered hand for even a single atomic task can take days. To address this, we introduce Dexterous Point Policy, a framework that learns dexterous manipulation policies directly from human videos and requires no robot demonstrations. Our core insight is that a unified 3D keypoint representation can bridge human and robot embodiments when used for both observations and actions. Specifically, we extract 3D keypoints of task-relevant objects and human hands from raw videos, and train an autoregressive transformer over these keypoints. We observe that at the keypoint level, specifically the wrist and fingertips, human and robot behaviors closely align, enabling direct policy transfer. On a suite of real-robot tasks spanning pick-and-place and tool use, Dexterous Point Policy attains 75.0% success, whereas a state-of-the-art VLA baseline reaches only 1.0%. Furthermore, our method generalizes strongly to unseen scenarios, including multi-object environments and novel object categories.

</details>


### [33] [Planar-Sector LOS Guidance for Interception of Agile Targets with Lifting-Wing Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10639)
*Linkai Liu,Kun Yang,Han Zou,Chen Min,Shuli Lv,Shuai Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种平面扇形视线（PS-LOS）制导框架，用于仅搭载单目相机的升力翼四旋翼无人机对敏捷空中目标的自主视觉拦截。与传统锥形视线约束不同，PS-LOS在紧凑约束横向图像误差的同时，在安全视场裕度内放松纵向图像误差，从而在保持目标可见性的同时释放更多机动能力（近LOS方向可用推力提升约50%）。结合延迟补偿状态估计和非线性制导控制架构，系统在长达138米的拦截距离和真实风扰下成功实现了对大幅、高频、不可预测运动目标的自主拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带strapdown相机的拦截方法使用锥形视线（LOS）约束将目标保持在图像中心附近，这种对称约束虽然安全但过度限制了机动性，减少了用于追击的有效推力。受FPV飞手在图像各方向不保持相等可见裕度的观察启发，本文旨在提出一种在保持可见性的同时释放机动能力的制导框架。

Method: 1) 提出平面扇形视线（PS-LOS）制导框架：横向紧凑约束图像误差，纵向在安全视场裕度内放松约束；2) 基于升力翼四旋翼模型分析PS-LOS相比锥形LOS的推力优势；3) 开发延迟补偿状态估计框架和非线性制导控制架构，实现仅基于LOS的无深度测量拦截。

Result: PS-LOS在LOS方向提供比传统锥形LOS约束多近50%的可用推力；户外飞行实验成功拦截了具有大幅、高频、不可预测运动的敏捷目标，在真实风扰下拦截距离达138米，全程保持连续视觉跟踪。

Conclusion: PS-LOS是一种既能保持可见性又能感知机动性的制导框架，有效解决了敏捷空中目标远程视觉拦截中可见性与机动性之间的强耦合问题。

Abstract: Autonomous visual interception of agile aerial targets is challenging due to unpredictable target motion, limited sensing, and the strong coupling between target visibility and interceptor maneuverability. Most existing strapdown-camera interception methods preserve visibility using conic line-of-sight (LOS) constraints that keep the target near the image center. While safe, such symmetric constraints unnecessarily restrict maneuverability and can significantly reduce the usable thrust for pursuit. Motivated by the observation that aggressive FPV pilots do not maintain equal visibility margins in all image directions, this paper proposes a Planar-Sector Line-of-Sight (PS-LOS) guidance framework for autonomous interception using a lifting-wing quadcopter equipped with only a strapdown monocular camera. PS-LOS tightly constrains lateral image error while relaxing longitudinal image error within a safe field-of-view margin, preserving visibility while releasing maneuverability for acceleration-intensive pursuit. Under the lifting-wing quadcopter model, PS-LOS provides nearly 50% more available thrust near the LOS direction than conventional conic LOS constraints. To realize LOS-only interception without direct depth measurements, a delay-compensated state-estimation framework and a nonlinear guidance-and-control architecture are developed for lifting-wing quadcopters. Extensive outdoor flight experiments demonstrate autonomous interception of agile targets exhibiting large-amplitude, high-frequency, and unpredictable motion under real wind disturbances. The proposed system achieves successful interceptions at ranges up to 138 m while maintaining continuous visual tracking throughout the engagement. The results validate PS-LOS as a visibility-preserving, maneuverability-aware guidance framework for long-range visual interception of agile aerial targets.

</details>


### [34] [UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683)
*Dong Fang,Youjun Wu,Yuanxin Zhong,Rui Zhang,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一灵巧手模型（UDHM）将人手和机器人手状态映射到共享22自由度语义接口，并基于此设计了UniDexTok——一种免重定向的状态分词器，通过学习嵌入条件离散令牌实现异质灵巧手的统一表示，将重建误差从厘米级降至亚毫米级（MPJAE降低98.98%，MPJPE降低99.03%），并展现出跨具身迁移和零样本/少样本学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手在硬件设计（运动学、关节定义、自由度）上存在巨大差异，导致难以像平行夹爪那样定义共享状态表示，使得灵巧手数据碎片化、难以联合训练。

Method: 提出UDHM将人手和机器人手状态映射到22自由度语义接口；基于UDHM设计UniDexTok，学习具身条件离散令牌，实现免重定向、不依赖仿真数据的统一表示。

Result: 相比UniHM基线，MPJAE从15.63°降至0.16°（降98.98%），MPJPE从18.51mm降至0.18mm（降99.03%），从厘米级提升至亚毫米级精度；跨具身数据能提升目标具身重建精度；具备零样本和少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDexTok有效解决了异质灵巧手的统一表示问题，实现了高精度重建和跨具身泛化，为灵巧手数据的联合训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands are essential for fine-grained manipulation, but their hardware designs vary substantially across embodiments. Differences in kinematics, joint definitions, and degrees of freedom make it difficult to define a shared state representation compared with parallel grippers. As a result, dexterous-hand data remains fragmented and difficult to use for joint training. Based on UDHM, we introduce UniDexTok, a retargeting-free state tokenizer that learns embodiment-conditioned discrete tokens from standardized real joint states. UniDexTok provides a unified representation for heterogeneous dexterous hands without relying on retargeting or simulation data. Compared with the recent baseline UniHM, UniDexTok reduces MPJAE from 15.63 degrees to 0.16 degrees and MPJPE from 18.51 mm to 0.18 mm, corresponding to error reductions of 98.98% and 99.03%, respectively. These results improve reconstruction from centimeter-scale to sub-millimeter accuracy. Experiments further show that data from other embodiments improves target-embodiment reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating the benefit of cross-embodiment tokenization. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的感知管线计算密集，但并非所有检测到的物体都对驾驶决策同等重要。聚焦于相关物体可以更高效地利用计算资源、降低处理延迟并减少感知噪声的传播。现有方法缺乏对物体相关性的高效、实时量化手段。

Method: 提出一种基于反事实分析的自监督方法：通过构建合成因果数据集，使用反事实推理（即假设物体不存在时驾驶行为的变化）来量化物体相关性，训练一个轻量级相关性模型实现毫秒级实时推理。

Conclusion: 反事实分析是一种有效的自监督方法用于量化物体相关性；该模型可用于实时感知选择、规划任务指导，并提升计算资源利用效率。相关模型和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous driving relies on computationally intensive perception pipelines to continuously detect and track objects in the surrounding environment. While some objects are key to plan safe and effective maneuvers, others may not be relevant and have no impact on the autonomous vehicle's driving decisions. Focusing on relevant objects allows a more efficient usage of available computational resources, reduces processing latencies, and limits the downstream propagation of perception noise. In this work, we propose a novel self-supervised approach based on counterfactual analysis to develop a relevance model - an AI-based tool that quantifies the relevance of objects for an autonomous vehicle. To demonstrate the potential of the proposed approach, we train a relevance model on a synthetic causal dataset generated in a selected urban scenario. Results show that the relevance model is able to accurately estimate the objects' relevance with millisecond-level latency, enabling real-time relevance estimation also in high-density scenarios. We also show that the relevance model can be used to build relevance heatmaps that offer valuable insights into the autonomous vehicle's driving policy and can be used to proactively inform perception and planning tasks. We openly release both the relevance model and the causal dataset.

Method: 将预测模型分为三个连续子模型：名义运动学自行车模型、离线贝叶斯线性回归(BLR)模型和在线稀疏高斯过程回归(SGPR)模型。通过高效融合历史数据和当前驾驶信息，在不大幅增加计算成本的前提下提供高精度预测和不确定性评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的组合式预测模型能够在实时条件下有效提升路径跟踪控制的精度和鲁棒性，为极限工况下的自动驾驶赛车提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous race cars, such as in Formula Student Driverless, operate close to their physical handling limits. The resulting highly nonlinear vehicle behavior increases the path tracking complexity, especially on narrow tracks. Model Predictive Control (MPC) is commonly used to address this issue, a method whose performance is closely tied to the accuracy of the underlying prediction model. This paper presents a novel, real-time capable prediction model for autonomous race cars that adjusts to changing conditions by combining information from past runs and the current driving situation. Our model is divided into three consecutive submodels: a nominal Kinematic Bicycle Model, an offline Bayesian Linear Regression (BLR) model, and an online Sparse Gaussian Process Regression (SGPR) model. The proposed approach enables efficient integration of all available data without significantly increasing computational cost, ensuring high prediction accuracy and a quantitative uncertainty assessment right from the start of the run. Compared to existing approaches, an improvement in prediction accuracy of up to 57% was achieved. Further, we successfully demonstrated the practical applicability of the model within an MPC-based path tracking controller on a real Formula Student race car.

</details>

*Tamara Bergerhoff,Sebastian Baader,Pascal Meißner,Frank Deinzer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种动态自适应速度规划方法，通过从车辆稳定性间接推断连续缩放因子替代轮胎-路面摩擦系数估计，生成连续摩擦图以实时优化目标速度。在真实大学生方程式赛车上测试，圈速提升最高35%，平均提升8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人驾驶赛车（如大学生方程式）中，高速下的速度规划需实时考虑赛道布局、环境影响、机械公差及控制误差，传统方法依赖轮胎-路面摩擦系数估计，但该系数难以准确获取且无法反映控制误差等综合影响。

Method: 不直接估计轮胎-路面摩擦系数，而是从车辆稳定性中间接推断连续缩放因子，该因子同时反映有效轮胎-路面交互和控制误差。基于此生成连续摩擦图，作为自适应基础计算最优目标速度。

Result: 在真实大学生方程式赛车上评估，10圈中圈速提升最高35%，相比非自适应方法平均提升8%。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应速度规划方法能有效应对动态变化的赛道和环境条件，显著提升圈速表现。

Abstract: In autonomous racing, especially in competitions such as Formula Student Driverless, precise planning of the target velocity of a race car is crucial for competitive lap times and stable driving behavior. Especially at high speeds, Velocity Planning (VP) is a significant challenge as it has to be performed in real time, taking into account track layouts, environmental influences, mechanical tolerances, and the resulting control inaccuracies. In this paper, we present a novel approach to VP that dynamically adapts to such changing conditions. Instead of estimating the physical Tire-Road Friction Coefficient (TRFC), a continuous scaling factor is inferred indirectly from vehicle stability. This factor not only reflects the effective tire-road interaction but also captures effects of control inaccuracies. From this, we generate a continuous friction map, which serves as a robust, adaptive basis for computing the optimal target speed, accounting for both vehicle and environmental limits. Our proposed approach was evaluated on a real Formula Student race car, showing a lap time improvement of 35 % over ten laps and an average increase of 8 % compared to a non-adaptive approach.

</details>
