### [60] [Anomaly Detection and Root Cause Analysis for Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09942)
*Luan Pham*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文针对微服务系统异常检测与根因分析(RCA)中存在的五大局限,提出两组贡献:第一组包括基于度量的端到端方法BARO、基于事件数据的方法EventADL、以及无需服务调用图的多模态RCA框架TORAI;第二组提供基准数据集RCAEval和系统评估框架,对现有RCA方法(尤其因果推断方法)进行了系统性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统的复杂性使得故障不可避免,导致用户体验下降和经济损失。现有异常检测与根因分析技术存在五大局限:1)将检测与RCA分离,检测不准确时效果差;2)过度关注指标、日志和追踪,忽略事件数据(如API调用、配置变更);3)多数方法依赖服务调用图;4)缺乏标准化数据集和评估框架;5)因果推断RCA的有效性、效率和鲁棒性不明确。

Method: 第一组方法:BARO(基于度量的端到端异常检测与RCA)、EventADL(事件数据的端到端框架)、TORAI(不需要服务调用图的多模态RCA框架)。第二组:RCAEval基准测试平台(提供即用数据集和可复现基线),以及针对现有RCA方法(特别是因果推断方法)的系统性评估。

Result: 在真实微服务系统上的大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。RCAEval提供了全面的基准和可复现的基线。对现有RCA方法的系统评估,特别是因果推断方法,提供了指导未来方向的见解。

Conclusion: 本论文推进了微服务故障的自动化异常检测与根因分析研究,为未来在事件缓解和修复方面的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Microservice systems are widely used to build cloud applications, yet their complexity makes failures inevitable, degrading user experience and causing economic loss. Automated anomaly detection and root cause analysis (RCA) are now active research areas, but existing techniques share five limitations. First, most treat anomaly detection and RCA separately, assuming anomalies are detected correctly, and falter when detection is imprecise due to noise or delay. Second, they focus on metrics, logs, and traces, leaving event data such as API calls and configuration changes underexplored. Third, many require a given service call graph and cannot diagnose without one. Fourth, the field lacks standardised datasets and evaluation frameworks, so methods are hard to compare fairly. Fifth, although causal inference-based RCA has become dominant, its effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness remain unclear.
  This thesis addresses these limitations through two groups of contributions. The first introduces methods that exploit observability data both independently and collectively. BARO is an end-to-end anomaly detection and RCA approach for metric data. EventADL is an end-to-end framework for event data. TORAI is a multimodal RCA framework that requires no service call graph. Extensive experiments on real microservice systems demonstrate their effectiveness and robustness. The second group delivers benchmarking datasets, an evaluation framework, and systematic evaluation efforts. RCAEval is a comprehensive benchmark providing ready-to-use datasets and reproducible baselines for future research. A systematic evaluation of existing RCA methods, especially causal inference-based approaches, offers insights that guide future directions. This thesis thereby advances automated anomaly detection and RCA for microservice failures, enabling future research on incident mitigation and remediation.

</details>


### [61] [Multi-task LLMs for Bug Classification: Efficient Inference with Auxiliary Decoding Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09956)
*Nikolai Rozanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的行级缺陷定位方法MLC，通过令牌对齐算法、多任务学习模型和优化训练策略，在保持与代理式方法相当性能的同时，将推理延迟降低数个数量级，每文件仅需生成一个令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动代码生成的缺陷验证方法存在严重不足：基于代理推理的方法成本高昂（需数分钟推理和数千令牌），函数级粒度不适用于精确调试；而行级轻量方法性能或上下文长度受限。

Method: (1) 令牌对齐算法解决先前工作中的分词挑战；(2) 轻量级多任务LLM（MLC）实现高效行级缺陷分类；(3) 针对多行预测的优化训练策略。

Result: 在全文件上下文行级缺陷定位中达到同类设置最先进水平；在Defects4J和PypiBugs基准上与代理式方法性能相当，但推理延迟降低数个数量级，每文件仅需单令牌生成；在Python域外小数据集上展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了行级精度与轻量高效的平衡，为LLM代码生成的验证提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of LLM-powered code generation has dramatically accelerated software development, yet effective verification methods remain severely underdeveloped. Existing bug localization techniques are either prohibitively expensive, requiring minutes of agentic reasoning and thousands of generated tokens per file, and/or operate at coarse function-level granularity unsuitable for precise debugging. While works that focus on line-level granularity and are more light-weight are often limited in their performance or context size. We introduce a novel line-level bug localization approach that addresses these limitations through three key contributions: (1) a token alignment algorithm that overcomes fundamental tokenization challenges in previous work, (2) a lightweight multi-task LLM for bug localization (MLC) enabling efficient line-level bug classification, and (3) an optimized training recipe for multi-line prediction. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance among similar setups on line-level bug localization with full-file context. At the same time we reach comparable performance to agentic approaches on Defects4J and PypiBugs benchmarks while reducing inference latency by orders of magnitudes, requiring only a single generated token per file. We further demonstrate strong generalization by introducing and evaluating on a small out-of-domain evaluation datasets in Python. We will open source our code, models, and datasets upon acceptance.

</details>


### [62] [Data-aware Static Analysis: Improving Detection of Semantic Faults in Machine Learning Code Using Data Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09957)
*Willem Meijer,Kristian Sandahl,Dániel Varró*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种数据感知的静态分析方法，用于在代码编写阶段而非模型训练后检测机器学习代码中的语义错误，通过结合数据流、控制流分析和API契约实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习开发者常在模型训练后通过手动分析结果才能发现语义错误（如未缩放数据用于缩放敏感模型），这个过程效率低下，需要一种能在编码阶段提前检测这些错误的方法。

Method: 提出数据感知的静态分析方法，结合数据流分析、控制流分析和API契约，在高层抽象上对机器学习代码进行数据感知推理。

Result: 分析真实世界机器学习notebook样本，证明该方法能够检测需要数据感知方法才能发现的语义错误。

Conclusion: 该方法有望让开发者在编写代码时而非训练模型后即发现语义错误，提高开发效率。

Abstract: Semantic faults specific to the use of machine learning models are a common problem for machine learning developers, causing suboptimal predictions, high computational cost, or incorrect outputs. For example, one may erroneously use unscaled data to train a scale-sensitive model. Machine learning developers detect these faults after training their models and manually analyzing the results, making it an inefficient process. We propose a novel data-aware static analysis approach to detect semantic faults in machine learning code, allowing developers to reveal these bugs while writing code instead of after training the model. Our approach uses combined data and control flow analysis, and API contracts, enabling data-aware reasoning about machine learning code at a high level of abstraction. We highlight the potential of our solution by analyzing a sample of real-world machine learning notebooks, finding that we can detect faults that require a data-aware approach.

</details>


### [63] [What makes a harness a harness: necessary and sufficient conditions for an agent harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10106)
*Sanderson Oliveira de Macedo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过概念分析构建了"智能体框架（agent harness）"的操作性定义，追溯了该术语从马具到测试框架再到机器学习评估框架的谱系，提出了一个包含必要充分条件的构成性定义，并通过对六个真实系统（Claude Code、Codex CLI、Aider、Cline、OpenHands、SWE-agent）的应用测试验证了该定义的一致包含与排除能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "agent harness"一词在生成式AI软件工程领域使用广泛但含义松散多义，缺乏一个能够一致包含和排除案例的参考定义，这阻碍了工程实践和智能体系统的科学比较。

Method: 结合具有持久标识符的学术著作与主要灰色文献（官方文档、术语表、工程报告）进行概念分析，重构术语谱系，提出构成性定义（必要充分条件），并将其操作化为包含/排除测试。

Result: 提出了一个操作性的agent harness定义，能够一致地区分agent harness与agent框架、SDK、IDE插件、评估框架、编排器等相邻概念，并在六个真实系统和刻意设计的边缘案例中验证了该测试的一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了agent harness的操作性定义和共享词汇表，能够指导工程实践和智能体系统的科学比较，并提出了以设计张力轴组织的研究议程。

Abstract: The term agent harness now circulates widely in software engineering with generative artificial intelligence. It names the layer that wraps a language model and turns it into a coding agent able to act on a repository. The usage is loose and polysemous. Sometimes the term denotes the whole product (Claude Code, Codex CLI); sometimes it denotes the evaluation scaffold that runs an agent against tasks (the SWE-bench harness); sometimes it gets conflated with an agent framework, an SDK, an IDE plugin, or an orchestrator. What is missing is a reference definition that works as an instrument, one that includes and excludes cases consistently. We build that definition through a conceptual analysis that combines works with persistent identifiers and primary grey-literature sources, such as official documentation, glossaries, and engineering reports. We reconstruct the genealogy of the term, from the horse's tack to the classic test harness, to the machine-learning evaluation harness, and finally to the agent harness. We then propose a constitutive definition that states the necessary and sufficient conditions for a system to be an agent harness, we operationalize it as an inclusion and exclusion test, and we draw the boundary of the concept against an agent framework, an agent SDK, an IDE plugin, an eval harness, and an orchestrator. We apply the definition to six real harnesses (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Aider, Cline, OpenHands, and SWE-agent) and to deliberate edge cases; the test includes and excludes consistently. We close with a research agenda organized by design tension axes. The contribution is an operational definition of agent harness, with a shared vocabulary, able to guide engineering practice and the scientific comparison of agentic systems.

</details>


### [64] [TestMap: Evidence Infrastructure for Foundation-Model-Assisted Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10211)
*Hunter Leary,Luke Hanuska,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TestMap，一个用于C#/.NET仓库的开源基础框架，可自动化进行证据支持的基础模型辅助测试生成，支持从仓库分析到实验追踪的全流程，并记录每个生成候选测试的生命周期（包括失败、修复、低影响和证据阳性结果），以提高生成测试的可观察性、可重复性和可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型能生成看似合理的单元测试，但判断这些测试是否正确、有用、可维护且值得集成非常困难。现有的验证过程分散在构建系统、测试运行器、覆盖率工具等多种工具中，缺乏统一的验证基础设施。

Method: TestMap通过集成仓库分析、源-测试映射、基准执行、代码度量收集、测试异味检测、覆盖率测量、变异测试、模型引导的测试生成、验证、修复及实验追踪等模块，构建了一个端到端的自动化验证流水线，并记录每个生成候选的完整生命周期。

Result: TestMap不仅报告最终通过的测试，还记录了每个生成候选的生命周期，包括失败、修复、低影响和证据阳性等中间结果，能揭示模型局限性、缺失上下文、修复成本、工具链低效或被测系统的潜在缺陷。

Conclusion: 以TestMap为设计案例，总结了使生成测试可观察、可重复和可比较所需的架构和证据模型，并指出了断言质量、度量归因、测试可维护性、脆弱性、执行成本和开发者接受度等开放挑战。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) can generate plausible unit tests, but determining whether those tests are correct, useful, maintainable, and worth integrating remains difficult. Generated tests must be mapped to the code they target, inserted into real projects, built, executed, measured against the baseline suite, repaired when necessary, and compared across models and generation strategies. This validation process is fragmented across build systems, test runners, coverage tools, mutation tools, static analyzers, and experiment scripts. The problem is especially important because generated tests are both code artifacts and validation artifacts: they must themselves be validated before they can be trusted as evidence about the system under test. This paper presents TestMap, an open-source infrastructure prototype that automates evidence-backed foundation-model-assisted test generation for C#/.NET repositories. TestMap supports repository analysis, source-test mapping, baseline execution, code metric collection, test smell detection, coverage measurement, mutation testing, model-guided test generation, validation, repair, and repository-specific experiment tracking. Rather than reporting only final passing tests, TestMap records the lifecycle of each generated candidate, including failed, repaired, low-impact, and evidence positive outcomes. These intermediate outcomes can reveal model limitations, missing context, repair cost, toolchain inefficiencies, or possible faults in the system under test. Using TestMap as a design case, we describe the architecture and evidence model needed to make generated tests observable, repeatable, and comparable across repositories, models, prompts, and generation strategies. We conclude with lessons learned and open challenges, including oracle and assertion quality, metric attribution, test maintainability, flakiness, execution cost, and developer acceptance.

</details>


### [65] [From Awareness to Action: How Developers Engage with Accessibility Innovation in LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10311)
*Thayssa Águila da Rocha,Luciane Silva,Ana Duarte,Marcelle Pereira Mota,Gustavo Pinto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对巴西一家科技公司的14个基于LLM的无障碍项目提案和9位参与者的焦点小组讨论进行分析，发现当由残障人士主导时，包容性创新更容易实现。研究认为，企业采用参与式方法可以将无障碍从合规要求转变为技术创新和文化变革的驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在企业环境中难以设计真正无障碍的数字解决方案，因为无障碍通常被视为合规要求而非创新机会。本研究旨在探索如何通过残障人士主导的参与式方法来推动包容性创新。

Method: 分析了14个基于LLM的无障碍项目提案，并组织了9位参与者（来自一家巴西科技公司）的焦点小组讨论。通过定性分析方法，研究无障碍创新在企业环境中的产生条件。

Result: 当无障碍倡议由残障人士亲自主导时，包容性创新更容易涌现。这表明参与式方法能够有效打破无障碍仅作为合规要求的局限。

Conclusion: 企业若采用类似的参与式方法，无障碍将从"事后补救"转变为推动技术卓越和文化变革的核心驱动力。

Abstract: Developers often struggle to design truly accessible digital solutions in corporate environments. In these environments, accessibility is usually treated as a compliance requirement rather than an innovation opportunity. By analyzing 14 LLM-based accessibility project proposals and focus group discussions with 9 participants at a Brazilian tech company, we found that inclusive innovation can emerge particularly when initiatives are led by People with Disabilities (PWD) themselves. If organizations adopt similar participatory approaches, accessibility would evolve from an afterthought into a driving force for technological excellence and cultural transformation.

</details>


### [66] [Communication Skills in Software Engineering: A Multivocal Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10320)
*Dannilo Rabelo,Deisy Peres,Emmanuel Dias,Thayssa Rocha,Enne Rebeca de Freitas,Kiev Gama,Gustavo Pinto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过多声部文献综述，发现学术和灰色文献都认为沟通是软件工程核心能力，但侧重点不同：学术界关注概念化和实证证据，灰色文献强调实践后果和行业实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中关于沟通技能的讨论分散在学术和灰色文献中，缺乏统一理解，限制了对其在教育与专业环境中的价值、教学和应用的全面认识。

Method: 采用多声部文献综述（Multivocal Literature Review）方法，综合学术与灰色文献进行分析。

Result: 学术与灰色来源在将沟通视为核心能力上高度一致，但侧重点不同：学术界聚焦概念化和实证证据，灰色文献强调实践后果和新兴行业实践。

Conclusion: 两种文献源存在收敛与分歧，提示需要更综合的视角来理解和应用软件工程中的沟通技能。

Abstract: Communication skills are increasingly recognized as essential in Software Engineering, yet discussions about them remain fragmented across academic and gray literature. This fragmentation is problematic because it limits a broader understanding of how communication is valued, taught, and applied in both educational and professional settings. Through a multivocal literature review, we found strong convergence between academic and gray sources in treating communication as a core competency, while also identifying differences in emphasis, with academia focusing on conceptualization and empirical evidence and gray literature stressing practical consequences and emerging industry practices.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond Coverage and Kill Scores: Empirically Measuring Test Suite Behavioural Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10417)
*Partha Protim Paul,Reid Holmes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从自然语言文档中提取方法级预期行为、并将其与现有测试用例映射的方法，用于检测测试套件的行为缺口。在对10个开源Java库的评估中，发现17.5%的预期行为完全未被测试，且传统结构覆盖指标（行覆盖、变异测试）无法预测这些缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试充分性指标（如代码覆盖率和变异测试）只衡量代码实现，无法检测代码的实际行为与其预期行为之间的差距，而此前缺乏可靠的方法来发现这些差距。

Method: 从自然语言文档和源代码中提取方法级预期行为，将其映射到现有测试用例，识别预期行为与已验证行为之间的缺口。在10个开源Java库（共8,922个方法）上进行评估，并对比了EVOSUITE和ASTER两类自动化测试生成工具。

Result: 提取了20,729个行为，精确率达93.1%；保守估计17.5%的预期行为完全未被测试（行为缺口）；EVOSUITE和ASTER分别有至少20.6%/27.1%的预期行为未被验证；大多数未测试行为出现在行覆盖率高和变异杀死率高的方法中。

Conclusion: 行为覆盖率是独立于传统结构覆盖指标的测试充分性维度，可以补充传统指标，因为传统指标无法预测行为缺口的存在。

Abstract: Traditional test adequacy metrics measure a system's implementation, not whether it adheres to its expected behaviour. While developers rely heavily on code coverage and mutation testing to assess test suite quality, these metrics are fundamentally implementation-centric and cannot detect gaps between what the code is expected to do and what it actually does. Unfortunately, there has been no way to reliably detect these discrepancies; in this paper we introduce an automated proof-of-concept approach to investigate these gaps. The approach extracts expected method-level behaviours from natural language documentation and source code, maps them to existing test cases, and identifies gaps between expected and validated behaviours. We evaluate the approach across ten popular open-source Java libraries comprising 8,922 methods, extracting 20,729 behaviours with 93.1% precision. Our empirical analysis conservatively estimates that 17.5% of detected expected behaviours remain entirely untested, which we term as the test suite's behavioural gap. To determine if these gaps are merely an artifact of human-driven testing, we evaluate state-of-the-art automated test generators (EVOSUITE / ASTER), finding that they similarly fail to validate at least 20.6% / 27.1% of detected expected behaviours. We further demonstrate that behavioural gaps are not predicted by traditional structural metrics: the majority of untested behaviours occur in methods that already have high line coverage, and over half persist in methods with high mutation kill score. These results suggest behavioural coverage acts as an independent dimension of test suite adequacy that can complement traditional structural metrics.

</details>


### [68] [MASTOR: A Multi-Agent Approach to Semantic Test Oracle Generation for RESTful APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10465)
*Sida Deng,Rubing Huang,Zhenzhen Yang,Man Zhang,Xuan Xie,Rongcun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MASTOR，一种基于多智能体的RESTful API语义测试预言生成方法，通过分析实现源码自动生成状态预言、字段预言和行为一致性预言，在13个开源项目上平均变异得分75.4%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RESTful API自动化测试方法仅依赖HTTP状态码和模式合规等简单检查，无法检测语义错误、业务逻辑违规和状态依赖不一致问题。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段通过源码提取智能体分析传递导入闭包构建每个端点的源码上下文；第二阶段包含两条并行预言生成路径——单操作路径生成状态预言和字段预言，多操作路径利用跨操作语义关联生成行为一致性预言；均经挑战者审核和预言规范化处理。

Result: 在WFD和PRAB数据集的13个开源项目（296个操作，251,303行代码）上，平均变异得分75.4%，生成10,022个预言。在50个操作的基线对比中，MASTOR比Direct Prompting高30.1个百分点（69.9% vs 39.8%），比SATORI高49.4个百分点（69.9% vs 20.5%）。

Conclusion: MASTOR通过源码分析和多智能体协作能有效生成语义测试预言，显著提升RESTful API的缺陷检测能力，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Existing automated RESTful API testing approaches commonly rely on simple checks (e.g., HTTP status codes, schema conformance), which are insufficient for detecting semantic faults, business logic violations, and state-dependent inconsistencies. To address this, we propose MASTOR, a Multi-Agent approach for generating Semantic Test Oracles for RESTful APIs based on implementation source code. MASTOR consists of two phases: source analysis and oracle generation. The former employs a source extraction agent to construct a source context for each endpoint operation by analyzing a transitive import closure of relevant source files. The latter employs two parallel oracle-generation paths over the collected contexts: a single-operation path producing status and field oracles per operation, and a multi-operation path generating behavioral consistency oracles for operation sequences by leveraging cross-operation semantic associations. Both paths apply a challenger-agent review, where a dedicated reviewer identifies weaknesses and issues improvement hints to guide targeted regeneration, followed by oracle normalization to filter out structurally invalid oracles. We evaluated MASTOR on a benchmark of 13 open-source RESTful API projects (296 operations, 251,303 lines of code) from the WFD and PRAB datasets. MASTOR achieved an average mutation score of 75.4%, generating 10,022 oracles. These oracles were translated into executable assertions via ToJUnit and ToPostmanAssertify, and into human-readable descriptions via ToReadable. In a baseline comparison on 50 selected operations, MASTOR outperformed Direct Prompting by 30.1 percentage points (69.9% vs. 39.8%) and SATORI by 49.4 percentage points (69.9% vs. 20.5%).

</details>


### [69] [Exploring and Complementing End Users' Requirements in IoT enabled System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10598)
*Haotian Li,Xiaohong Chen,Zhi Jin,Shuyuan Xiao,Chenxu Wang,Haoxiang Yan,Xiaoyi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对用户创建的IoT自动化规则存在表达碎片化、缺乏高层意图的问题，本文提出了一种基于意图的需求补全方法，通过双向需求追溯树将规则补全重构为"从碎片规则重建意图→从意图再生规则"的双过程，并设计了结合LLM推理与结构化追溯的多智能体框架，在规则补全率上提升43%，逻辑冲突减少21%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最终用户通过触发器-动作编程创建IoT自动化规则时，其表达往往是碎片化的，仅描述设备操作而非高层意图。这种意图与规则之间的鸿沟导致规则缺少条件、存在逻辑冲突、忽略安全约束，可能引发危险行为。

Method: 提出意图驱动的需求补全方法，将规则补全重构为双过程：(1) 从碎片化规则重建意图；(2) 从意图再生规则，并在全过程中嵌入安全保障。具体设计包括：三层双向需求追溯树（规则-意图-质量关注点），以及结合LLM推理与结构化追溯的多智能体框架。

Result: 该方法显著优于基线，规则补全率提升43%，逻辑冲突减少21%以上。

Conclusion: 通过将规则补全建立在意图理解之上，实现了从用户责任到系统责任、从功能正确性到整体可信赖性的范式转变。

Abstract: End users create IoT automation rules via trigger action programming, but their expressions are often fragmented, capturing device operations rather than high level intents. This gap leads to missing conditions, logical conflicts, and overlooked safety constraints, risking hazardous behaviors. To address this, we propose an intent driven requirements completion approach that reframes rule completion as a dual process: reconstructing intent from fragmented rules, then regenerating rules from that intent, with safety embedded throughout. We introduce a Bidirectional Requirements Traceability Tree, a three layer model linking rules, intents, and quality concerns, and design a multiagent framework that combines LLM reasoning with structured traceability. This enables completions that are both functionally complete and inherently safe, while remaining traceable and explainable. Evaluation shows our method significantly outperforms the baselines, improving the rule completion rate by 43% and reducing logical conflicts by over 21%. By grounding completion in intent understanding, we shift the paradigm from user to system responsibility, and from functional correctness to holistic trustworthiness.

</details>


### [70] [Watts and Debts of Agentic Frameworks: An Empirical Study (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10702)
*Aneetta Sara Shany,Chandrasekar S,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一项实证研究，旨在探索智能体AI系统中自承认技术债务（SATD）与运行时硬件级能耗之间的相关性，通过评估五个开源智能体框架，验证代码质量能否作为能源效率的早期预警指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI系统存在技术债务和运行时能耗两大隐藏风险，但两者之间的经验性联系尚未被探索，导致开发者和组织在管理可持续性和运营预算时存在盲区。

Method: 在严格受控环境中对五个开源智能体框架执行标准化任务集；通过自动化Python注释挖掘和基于LLM的微调提示分类提取SATD；在硬件层面测量运行时能耗；回答三个研究问题：框架中TD的存在性、跨架构运行时能耗差异、TD与任务级能耗的统计相关性。

Result: 论文目前为研究提案阶段，尚未给出具体实验结果；预期结果将建立自动化源代码分析能否作为能源效率框架选择的可靠代理指标。

Conclusion: 研究结论将推动绿色软件工程和智能体AI质量研究的发展，验证代码质量分析能否作为能源感知设计决策的依据。

Abstract: Context: Every agentic AI system shipped to production carries two hidden risks: accumulated Technical Debt (TD) and unmonitored runtime energy costs. While functional benchmarking is common, the empirical link between internal structural quality (specifically TD) and dynamic energy consumption during execution remains unexplored, creating a blind spot for practitioners and organizations managing sustainability and operational budgets at scale. Goal: We propose a confirmatory empirical study correlating Self-Admitted Technical Debt (SATD) with hardware-level runtime energy consumption across agentic frameworks, to determine whether code quality can drive energy-aware design decisions. Method: We will evaluate five open-source agentic frameworks by executing a standardized task suite in a strictly controlled environment. SATD will be extracted via automated Python-based comment mining and categorized via LLM-based classification using fine-tuned prompt, while runtime energy will be measured at the hardware level. Our study will investigate three core research questions: (RQ1) the presence of TD within these frameworks; (RQ2) the variance in runtime energy consumption across architectures; and (RQ3) the statistical correlation between a framework's TD and its task-level energy consumption. Conclusion: The findings will establish whether automated source code analysis can serve as a reliable, early-warning proxy for energy-efficient framework selection, thereby advancing both green software engineering and agentic AI quality research.

</details>


### [71] [DeNovoSWE: Scaling Long-Horizon Environments for Generating Entire Repositories from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10728)
*Jiale Zhao,Guoxin Chen,Fanzhe Meng,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeNovoSWE，一个包含4,818个高质量实例的大规模全仓库生成数据集，通过自动化的沙箱智能体工作流构建，采用分治与批评-修复策略以及难度感知的轨迹过滤策略。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上微调后，在BeyondSWE-Doc2Repo基准上的得分从5.8%提升至47.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代码智能体能力不断提升，其预期角色正从局部bug修复扩展到从高层规范设计并实现完整软件仓库。然而，由于缺乏大规模、可验证的全仓库生成训练数据，训练智能体完成此类长周期软件工程任务仍十分困难。

Method: 通过精心设计的沙箱化智能体工作流自动构建数据集，无需人工标注；采用分治与批评-修复（divide and conquer, critic-repair）策略；引入难度感知的轨迹过滤策略以平衡数据质量与多样性。

Result: 微调Qwen3-30B-A3B后，在BeyondSWE-Doc2Repo基准上得分从5.8%提升至47.2%，显著改善了长周期软件工程任务性能。

Conclusion: DeNovoSWE数据集能够有效提升LLM代码智能体在完整仓库生成任务上的能力，证明了自动化数据构建策略在大规模长周期软件工程训练数据生成中的可行性。

Abstract: As the capabilities of LLM-based code agents continue to advance, their expected role is expanding beyond localized bug fixing in existing codebases toward architecting and implementing complete software repositories from high-level specifications. However, training agents for such long-horizon software engineering tasks remains difficult due to the scarcity of large-scale, verifiable whole-repository generation data. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{DeNovoSWE}, a large-scale dataset for whole-repository generation. DeNovoSWE comprises 4,818 high-quality instances, where each instance requires generating a complete repository from documentation. Our dataset is automatically constructed through a carefully designed sandboxed agentic workflow, enabling scalable curation without human annotation. DeNovoSWE is constructed with "divide and conquer" and critic-repair philosophy. To balance data quality and diversity, we further introduce a difficulty-aware trajectory filtering strategy. Fine-tuning Qwen3-30B-A3B on DeNovoSWE substantially improves long-horizon SWE performance, raising its score on the challenging BeyondSWE-Doc2Repo benchmark from 5.8% to 47.2%.

</details>


### [72] [Modular2Simple: A Tool for Modular Scenario Creation Based on the OpenSCENARIO Format](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10851)
*Nikolai Khriapov,Mohamed Taha Drif,Renjue Li,Cas Widdershoven*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Modular2Simple是一个与CARLA模拟器集成、支持OpenSCENARIO格式的工具，通过组合简单场景来创建模块化复杂场景，简化了自动驾驶系统测试场景的创建过程，降低了开发时间和代码复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统（ADS）的快速发展需要大量真实且复杂的场景用于测试和验证，但现有场景创建方法复杂且耗时，亟需一种简化场景创建流程的工具。

Method: Modular2Simple通过利用现有OpenSCENARIO格式的简单场景，允许开发者将多个简单或模块化场景组合成可定制的模块化场景，并与CARLA模拟器无缝集成。

Result: 该工具显著降低了场景开发的复杂度和时间成本，提升了场景复用性和可定制性，相比传统方法在场景设计和测试效率上有明显改进。

Conclusion: Modular2Simple有效解决了ADS场景创建的复杂性问题，兼具灵活性与效率，适用于支持OpenSCENARIO格式的各种软件平台。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of autonomous driving systems (ADS) has introduced significant challenges, particularly in the creation of realistic and complex scenarios for testing and validation. This paper introduces Modular2Simple, a tool designed to address these challenges by simplifying and enhancing the process of creating complex ADS scenarios. Modular2Simple seamlessly integrates with the CARLA simulator and is applicable to any software that supports the OpenSCENARIO format. By leveraging existing simple scenarios in the OpenSCENARIO format, the tool enables developers to create easily customizable modular scenarios through the combination of multiple simple or modular scenarios, significantly simplifying the scenario creation process while maintaining flexibility in scenario design. This approach not only facilitates the development of complex scenarios, reducing both development time and effort, but also promotes scenario reuse and customization, which leads to a significant reduction in code complexity and enhanced efficiency in scenario design and testing compared to traditional scenario development methods.

</details>


### [73] [From Perception to Action: Can UI Interventions Foster Sustainable LLM Chatbot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10861)
*Nitish Patkar,Pooja Rani,Jack Glässer,Simon Lüscher,Martin Kropp*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过用户界面层面的可持续性干预措施（如节能模式切换、能耗反馈等）来提高用户对LLM聊天机器人能源消耗的认知，并鼓励更节能的使用行为。通过基线调查（77人）和为期5天的实地研究（11人）发现，节能模式占使用量的55.8%，用户主要通过模式切换而非缩短提示词来节能，表明UI干预能有效促进能源负责任的使用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM聊天机器人的能源消耗日益成为可持续性关注焦点，现有缓解策略多集中于模型或基础设施效率优化，而用户界面层作为影响交互行为的潜在杠杆尚未被充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究UI层面的可持续性干预能否在不降低可用性的前提下提升用户能源意识并鼓励节能行为。

Method: 首先进行77人的基线调查评估用户对AI能源消耗的认知水平和干预概念接受度；基于说服技术与选择架构理论，实现了一个具有三模式开关（节能/平衡/性能）、每次响应能耗反馈、发送前能耗预估、用量仪表盘和能耗类比的可聊天机器人原型；随后开展为期5天、11名参与者的实地研究评估干预效果。

Result: 基线调查中94.8%受访者报告对AI能耗有认知，但88.3%错误估计实际消耗；仅39.0%愿意为节能接受性能折衷。实地研究中节能模式占55.8%的提示量，90.9%自我报告在不需要高精度时主动选择节能模式；用户并未缩短提示长度，说明模式切换是主要行为机制。

Conclusion: 面向可持续性的UI干预能有效提升用户能源意识并支持更节能的LLM聊天机器人交互模式，其效果最好被理解为行为层面和基于模型估计的补充性方案，可与后端效率优化工作互补。提供的原型和复现包支持未来对能源感知对话式AI设计的进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-powered chatbots are increasingly embedded in everyday workflows, raising sustainability concerns due to their energy use. Most mitigation strategies emphasize model or infrastructure efficiency, while the user-interface (UI) layer remains underexplored despite its potential to shape interaction behavior. We investigate whether sustainability-oriented UI interventions can increase users' energy awareness and encourage more energy-responsible chatbot use without reducing usability. We first conducted a baseline survey with 77 participants to assess awareness and receptiveness to intervention concepts. Guided by prior work on persuasive technology and choice architecture, we implemented a web-based chatbot prototype with a three-mode switch (Energy-efficient, Balanced, Performance), per-response energy feedback, pre-send energy estimates, a usage metrics dashboard, and energy analogies. We then evaluated the prototype in a five-day field study with 11 participants. In the baseline survey, 94.8% of respondents reported at least some awareness of AI energy use, yet 88.3% misestimated actual consumption. Although concern about environmental impact was high, only 39.0% indicated willingness to accept a performance trade-off for lower energy use. In the field study, Energy-efficient mode accounted for 55.8% of logged prompts, while 90.9% self-reported actively choosing Eco-mode when high accuracy was not required. Participants did not reduce prompt length, suggesting mode switching as the primary behavioral mechanism. Sustainability-oriented UI interventions can improve awareness and support more energy-responsible interaction patterns in LLM chatbots. These effects are best interpreted as behavioral and model-based estimates that complement backend efficiency work, and the provided prototype and replication package support further research on energy-aware conversational AI design.

</details>


### [74] [Writing Better Software Explanations: A Guideline-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10880)
*Martin Obaidi,Jean-Carl Kremser,Hannah Deters,Jakob Droste,Marc Herrmann,Kurt Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指南的软件解释表述支持工具，结合LLM辅助文本生成与经验性质量指南，通过生成、质量检查和迭代修订的结构化流程帮助开发者撰写软件解释。两阶段评估表明，工具支持下的表述平均提速24.4%，且用户满意度显著高于纯人工方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统越来越依赖自然语言解释来满足用户的需求沟通和支持，但确保这些解释的一致性、相关性和良好表述仍是重大挑战。纯粹的LLM自动生成缺乏可靠的 grounding 和可控的输出质量。

Method: 提出一种基于指南的表述支持工具，将写作过程结构化为生成、质量检查和迭代修订三个阶段，同时保持开发者的领域控制。采用两阶段评估：第一阶段为6名行业从业者的访谈式开发实验（对比纯人工与LLM辅助条件）；第二阶段为17名参与者的204对配对比较用户调查。

Result: 工具辅助表述平均速度提升24.4%（但推理性分析仅显示出效率趋势）。用户调查中，工具支持的解释在总体满意度上显著高于纯人工解释（p=0.003, rank-biserial correlation=0.86）。

Conclusion: 指南驱动的LLM辅助具有潜在效率提升和更高的表述质量感知。未来工作应考察长期工业使用及与现有开发工作流的集成。

Abstract: As software systems increasingly rely on natural-language explanations to address user-reported explanation needs in requirements communication and support, ensuring that such explanations are consistent, relevant, and well formulated remains a major challenge. Purely automatic large language model (LLM) generation often lacks reliable grounding and controllable output quality. In this paper, we present a guideline-based formulation support tool for software explanations that combines LLM-assisted text generation with an empirically derived quality guideline. The tool structures the writing process into generation, quality checking, and iterative revision, while keeping domain control with developers. We evaluated the approach in a two-phase study consisting of an interview-based developer experiment and a controlled user survey. Six industry practitioners with software development or DevOps experience formulated explanations for real explanation needs in a human-only manual condition and in a human-with-LLM-support condition. In this small-scale evaluation, tool-supported formulation was on average 24.4% faster, although inferential analyses indicated only a trend for efficiency. In a subsequent user study with 17 participants and 204 paired comparisons, tool-supported explanations were rated significantly higher in overall satisfaction than manual explanations (p=0.003, rank-biserial correlation=0.86). Our findings suggest potential efficiency gains and higher perceived formulation quality through guideline-driven LLM assistance. Future work should examine long-term industrial use and integration into existing development workflows.

</details>


### [75] [From Quality Properties to Practice: A Guideline and Workflow for Explainability Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10882)
*Martin Obaidi,Jakob Droste,Hannah Deters,Marc Herrmann,Michel Krahl,Kurt Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指导原则的序列化工作流来编制可解释性需求，并通过文献综述、开发者访谈和问卷调查提炼出十条核心质量属性。将该工作流工具化后，通过两项用户研究（需求工程师n=6，软件开发者n=18）验证，发现工具支持可将编制时间平均减少23.5%，且工具生成的需求在可实现性和编制质量上与手动编写版本相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI软件系统中的可解释性需求往往被随意编写，且缺乏指导的大语言模型支持会产生模糊、不一致或不完整的表述，亟需一种系统化的方法来保证可解释性需求的质量。

Method: 通过结构化文献综述和开发者访谈收集候选质量属性；通过在线问卷调查（n=20）对这些属性进行优先级排序，提炼出十条核心属性及可操作编制指南；将指南工具化为Web应用，支持编排、属性检查与修订的迭代工作流；通过两项研究（需求工程师工作坊n=6；独立在线研究n=18）评估效果。

Result: 工具支持将需求编制时间平均减少23.5%（Wilcoxon p=0.021）；工具支持与手动编写的需求在可实现性和编制质量上评分相当，且描述性统计上略偏向工具支持版本。

Conclusion: 将优先化的质量指南与轻量级LLM支持相结合，可以在减少编制工作量的同时，产生与手动编写质量相当的可解释性需求。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly required in AI-enabled software systems to support transparency, user trust, and compliance. Yet, explainability requirements are often written ad hoc, and unguided large language model support can yield vague, inconsistent, or incomplete statements. This paper presents a sequential, guideline-driven workflow for formulating explainability requirements and evaluates its tool-based operationalization. We first elicited candidate quality properties through a structured literature review and developer interviews. We then prioritized these properties in an online survey with practitioners (n=20) and derived a concise guideline of ten core properties with actionable formulation instructions. Next, we operationalized the guideline in a web-based tool that supports an iterative workflow of drafting, property-based checks, and revision. We evaluated the workflow in two complementary studies. In a workshop with requirements engineers (n=6), tool support reduced formulation time by 23.5% on average (Wilcoxon p=0.021). In an independent online study with software developers (n=18), tool-supported and manually written requirements received comparable ratings for implementability and formulation quality, with a descriptive slight preference tendency toward the tool-supported versions. Overall, our results suggest that combining a prioritized quality guideline with lightweight LLM support can reduce formulation effort while producing requirements that are perceived comparably to manually written ones.

</details>


### [76] [Early Comparative Evaluation of Transformer Models for Multilingual Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10925)
*Fiza Naseer,Javad Khan,Muhammad Yaqoob,Alexios Mylonas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了BERT、RoBERTa和CodeBERT在HTML、Python、JavaScript和PHP四种语言的二进制漏洞检测中的表现，结果表明跨语言漏洞检测需要更语言感知和鲁棒的Transformer建模策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用通常结合多种编程语言，而现有漏洞检测方法对多语言场景的支持不足，因此需要评估不同Transformer模型在跨语言二进制漏洞检测中的表现差异。

Method: 使用CVEFixes数据集，对BERT、RoBERTa和CodeBERT三种模型在HTML、Python、JavaScript和PHP四种语言上进行二进制漏洞检测，采用语言感知的三折分层交叉验证。

Result: 不同语言上的检测性能存在显著差异，表明现有Transformer模型在跨语言场景下表现不一致。

Conclusion: 多语言漏洞检测需要更语言感知和鲁棒的Transformer建模策略，以应对不同语言间的性能差异。

Abstract: Software vulnerability detection is increasingly important as modern applications combine multiple programming languages. This paper presents an early comparative evaluation of BERT, RoBERTa, and CodeBERT for binary vulnerability detection across HTML, Python, JavaScript, and PHP using the CVEFixes dataset and language-wise three-fold stratified cross-validation. The results show clear performance differences across languages, indicating that multilingual vulnerability detection requires more language-aware and robust transformer-based modelling strategies.

</details>


### [77] [GapFuzz: Cross-Plane Divergence Fuzzing for Distributed SDN Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11035)
*Moustapha Awwalou Diouf,Samuel Ouya,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GapFuzz是一个针对分布式SDN集群的状态并发模糊测试工具，通过在主节点和备份节点间注入冲突的北向请求并检测跨平面状态不一致，在ONOS 2.7三节点集群上实现了81.7%的冲突检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式SDN集群中主备节点异步复制流状态会产生竞争窗口，导致备份节点提交冲突规则、主节点序列化到数据平面、内核数据路径锁定无权威参考的动作。现有SDN模糊测试工具或局限于控制平面、或针对单控制器、或未引导并发以触发复制竞争，无法发现此类故障。

Method: 1) 在两个非主节点注入成对冲突北向请求，控制注入间隔Δt；2) 通过查询所有副本和内核数据路径动作重建全局跨平面状态；3) 两阶段时序搜索：检测是否存在分歧，然后倍增并二分法约束注入时间窗口；4) 生命周期探针标记每个判定为瞬态或持久态，归入源自ONOS 2.7源码的四类跨平面状态类。

Result: 在三节点ONOS 2.7集群上，GapFuzz在81.7%的尝试中产生分歧判定（N=50, Wilson 95% CI [77.3, 85.4]%）；所有分歧位于集群权威状态与内核数据路径之间。第二阶段将其中一个模板的竞争窗口缩小至5ms，其余六个模板处于倍增上限模式（Δt_max=10.24s），99.4%的分歧持续超过30秒。用OpenFlow用户空间探针替换内核数据路径探针后，整体检测率下降26.6个百分点，排除规范化强制判定后下降46.5个百分点。

Conclusion: GapFuzz有效检测分布式SDN集群中的跨平面状态不一致问题，揭示了现有模糊测试工具因依赖用户空间探针而严重低估此类故障的严重性，强调内核数据路径探针对检测此类竞争条件不可或缺。

Abstract: Distributed Software-Defined Networking (SDN) clusters replicate flow state asynchronously between a master node and its backups, leaving a window during which two backup nodes can each commit a contradictory rule, the master can serialize both into the data plane, and the kernel datapath can latch onto an action that no node believes authoritative. Existing SDN fuzzers miss this fault: they confine their oracle to the control plane, target a single controller, or do not steer concurrency to provoke replication races.
  We present GapFuzz, a stateful concurrency fuzzer for distributed SDN clusters. GapFuzz injects pairs of contradictory Northbound requests on two non-master nodes with controlled inter-injection delay $Δt$, and reconstructs the global cross-plane state by querying every replica and the kernel-datapath action through ovs-appctl ofproto/trace. A two-phase timing search detects whether a divergence exists, then doubles and bisects on $Δt$ to bound the injection-time window; a lifetime probe labels each verdict transient or persistent and assigns it to one of four cross-plane state classes derived from the ONOS 2.7 source.
  On a three-node ONOS 2.7 cluster, GapFuzz produces a divergent verdict in 81.7% of attempts ($N=50$, Wilson 95% CI $[77.3, 85.4]$%); every divergence sits between the cluster's authoritative state and the kernel datapath. Phase 2 separates a 5 ms race window for one template from a doubling-cap regime ($Δt_{\max}=10.24$ s) for six others, and 99.4% of divergences persist past 30 s. Replacing the kernel-datapath probe with the OpenFlow user-space probe used by prior fuzzers drops detection by 26.6 percentage points overall and by 46.5 points after excluding canonicalization-forced verdicts.

</details>


### [78] [Making Software Meaningful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11051)
*Eagon Meng,Abutalib Namazov,Carmel Schare,Alcino Cunha,Daniel Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过采用单一度量（对软件行为的明确意义表示）来提升软件可用性、模块化和可问责性，并将行为现象组织为词汇、个体、动作和事实，通过概念划分实现意义单元化，在设计、LLM代码生成和智能体问责三个领域展示了应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件在可用性、模块化和可问责性方面存在不足，缺乏一种统一的、明确的意义表示来支撑所有利益相关者之间的沟通以及工件与活动的组织。

Method: 提出以"明确意义承诺"为核心的方法，构建领域行为表示，将现象分为个体、动作和事实，并通过动作划分形成概念单元。

Result: 在三个领域展示了方法有效性：1) 通过共享意义对齐用户与设计者以提升可用性；2) 将意义单元映射为代码单元，借助LLM生成模块化且可读的代码；3) 通过行为准则定义智能体的预期行为实现可问责性。

Conclusion: 单一维度（明确意义）能够在多个软件工程维度上产生协同改进效果，为软件设计与开发提供统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Adopting a single measure can improve the usability, modularity and accountability of software: a commitment to explicit meaning. This entails constructing and agreeing upon a representation of the behavior of the software, as observed in the domain of application. The phenomena comprising this behavior become a vocabulary that grounds all discourse about the software, among all stakeholders, and for all artifacts and activities. These phenomena are individuals; actions they participate in; and facts that result from actions. They can be organized, by partitioning the set of actions, into concepts, offering larger units of meaning. Examples of exploiting meaning are given in three areas: designing for usability (by aligning user and designer on a single shared meaning); generating modular code with LLMs (by mapping units of meaning to units of code, achieving not only modularity but also legibility); and making agents accountable (by having them adhere to a code of conduct that defines their intended behavior).

</details>


### [79] [Operationalizing Property-Based Testing for Data-Intensive Scalable Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11132)
*Yaoxuan Wu,Ingrid Lee,Ahmad Humayun,Muhammad Ali Gulzar,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DiscPBT是一个针对Apache Spark的基于属性的测试引擎，通过引入八个可重用的元属性（等价重写、数据分解、计算分解和算子局部语义关系），并结合可重用的工作流骨架和输入数据生成器，有效检测DISC框架中的语义漂移和优化相关错误。实验表明，相比CometFuzz，DiscPBT在分支覆盖率（1.2倍）和计划多样性（1153倍）上均有显著提升，并能发现崩溃式模糊测试无法捕捉的跨版本语义漂移和边界情况缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊测试在捕获DISC框架中的语义漂移和优化相关错误方面能力不足，而基于属性的测试（PBT）虽然能通过检查通用语义不变量来弥补这一缺陷，但如何系统化地将PBT应用于DISC系统仍然困难，既需要可重用的属性定义，又需要有效的实例化方法。

Method: DiscPBT引入了八个可重用的元属性，涵盖等价重写、数据分解、计算分解和算子局部语义关系；同时提供可重用的生成器用于合成有效的工作流骨架和输入数据，以及一个实例化框架，通过兼容的算子、表达式和UDF在模式兼容的上下文中实现每个元属性。

Result: 在PySpark上的评估显示，DiscPBT相比CometFuzz实现了1.2倍的分支覆盖率和1153倍的计划多样性。在66个具体属性中，DiscPBT发现了跨版本语义漂移以及涉及NaN和空输入的细微边界情况缺陷，这些都是基于崩溃的模糊测试无法捕获的。

Conclusion: 系统化的基于属性测试对于揭示DISC框架中的语义问题具有重要价值。

Abstract: While fuzzing effectively catches crashes, its shallow oracles often miss semantic drifts and optimization-related errors in data-intensive scalable computing (DISC) frameworks. Property-based testing (PBT) addresses this limitation by checking general semantic invariants across diverse workloads and inputs, rather than relying on specific expected outputs. However, systematically operationalizing PBT for DISC systems remains difficult because it requires both reusable property definitions and effective instantiation into valid workloads and data.
  We present DiscPBT, a property-based testing engine for Apache Spark. DiscPBT introduces eight reusable meta-properties for DISC semantic testing, spanning equivalence rewriting, data decomposition, computation decomposition, and operator-local semantic relations. To operationalize these meta-properties, DiscPBT provides reusable generators for synthesizing valid workload skeletons and input data, together with an instantiation framework that realizes each meta-property in schema-compatible contexts through compatible operators, expressions, and UDFs.
  Our evaluation on PySpark shows that DiscPBT achieves 1.2$\times$ higher branch coverage and 1153$\times$ greater plan diversity than CometFuzz. Across 66 concrete properties, DiscPBT reveals cross-version semantic drift as well as subtle corner-case pitfalls involving NaN and empty inputs, that are not captured by crash-based fuzzing alone. These results demonstrate the value of systematic PBT for uncovering semantic issues in DISC frameworks.

</details>

### [80] [Safecloud: A Distributed, Encrypted Storage Cloud for Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09870)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Safecloud 是一个分布式加密存储和流媒体网络，通过单根密钥层级、收敛内容寻址、三棵并行树结构、密钥树兼作流索引以及基于 Proof-of-Corruption 的经济激励机制，实现端到端加密、去重、完整性验证和高效流媒体传输，且存储节点无法访问明文或持有密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化存储方案（如 Filecoin）在机密性、加密密钥管理、流媒体支持和成本效率方面存在不足。Safecloud 旨在构建一个存储和路由节点永远无法看到明文或持有密钥的分布式加密存储与流媒体网络，同时提供高效的去重、完整性验证和经济的激励机制。

Method: (1) 单根密钥层级：所有密钥通过 HKDF 从单一根密钥确定性派生，所有者与授权受让人推导出相同的块密钥（派生一致性），子树密钥仅派生其自身范围（委托隔离）；(2) 收敛内容寻址：相同内容产生相同密文和标识符，实现无需明文暴露的去重，标识符绑定认证密文使无密钥的 Drop 可验证完整性（盲可验证性）；(3) 在同一导航路径上构建三棵并行树（Merkle 树保证完整性、密钥派生树保证机密性、访问树保证授权），实现可靠的 Merkle 验证检索；(4) 密钥树兼作流索引：播放器 O(1) 推导每段密钥，通过派生实现定位，并行轨道（视频、音频、字幕）作为独立子树可按轨道和按段解锁；(5) Jets 和 Drops 通过单签名存储证明挑战（Proof-of-Corruption）赚取 Safebux，采用零和经济模型，比 Filecoin 的复制证明密封更便宜。

Result: 给出了完整的架构设计、密码学构造、威胁模型以及开源参考实现，并明确区分了已实现部分与设计部分。

Conclusion: Safecloud 提供了一种兼顾机密性、去重、完整性验证、高效流媒体和经济激励的分布式加密存储方案，在密钥管理和存储证明成本上优于现有方案（如 Filecoin）。

Abstract: We present Safecloud, a distributed, encrypted, self-pricing storage and streaming network whose storage and routing nodes never see plaintext and never hold keys. Each file is split into chunks, encrypted on the owner's device, and distributed across Drops (browser tabs storing ciphertext in IndexedDB) and Jets (federated routing servers). Only the owner, or an authorised grantee, can decrypt. We make five contributions: (1) A one-root key hierarchy: every key derives deterministically from a single root via HKDF, and owner and range-scoped grantee derive identical chunk keys (derivation agreement); a subtree key derives its range and nothing else (delegation containment). (2) Convergent content addressing: identical content yields identical ciphertext and identifiers, enabling deduplication without plaintext exposure, with identifiers binding authenticated ciphertext so a keyless Drop verifies integrity (blind verifiability). (3) Three parallel trees over one navigation path (Merkle for integrity, key-derivation for confidentiality, access for authorisation), with sound Merkle-verified retrieval. (4) The key tree doubles as a streaming index: a player derives each segment key in O(1), seeking by derivation, while parallel tracks (video, audio, captions) are independent subtrees unlockable per-track and per-segment, a combination we believe no prior encrypted-storage network offers. (5) Jets and Drops earn Safebux verifiably, kept honest by a one-signature proof-of-storage challenge under chilling-effect Proof-of-Corruption, a zero-sum economy that is significantly cheaper than Filecoin's proof-of-replication sealing (which is slow and provides no confidentiality). We give the architecture, cryptographic construction, a threat model, and an open-source reference implementation, stating precisely what is implemented versus designed.

</details>


### [81] [IDP-Bench: Benchmarking ability of LLMs to protect personal information in interdependent privacy contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09908)
*Ayana Hussain,Soumya Sharma,Golnoosh Farnadi,Nicholas Vincent,Héber Hwang Arcolezi,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IDP-Bench，首个针对大语言模型在 interdependent privacy（相互依赖隐私）场景下的评估基准，基于情境完整性框架构建。实验发现8个开源LLM在共同所有权识别上表现良好（6/8超过90%），但在识别CI参数和信息主体、以及判断分享适当性上普遍存在不足（7/8低于74%，5/8低于77%），且小模型性能下降、提示敏感性高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私研究主要关注个体层面的隐私风险，忽视了 interdependent privacy（IDP）——即一个人的数据可能被他人无意中泄露的问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立首个专门评估LLM对IDP场景理解能力的基准。

Method: 基于Contextual Integrity（CI）框架构建IDP-Bench基准，包含多个IDP场景，评估LLM在三个层次的IDP推理能力。使用两个LLM作为评判者，对8个开源LLM进行测评。

Result: 1）6/8模型在共同所有权识别上超过90%；2）7/8模型在识别CI参数（信息属性、主要主体）和IDP特定参数（次要主体）上得分低于74%；3）5/8模型在判断分享适当性上得分低于77%；4）模型规模越大分享判断能力越好，小模型性能下降；5）IDP相关问题存在高度提示敏感性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM对IDP场景的理解存在显著不足，尤其在识别关键隐私参数和判断信息分享是否适当方面。这提示LLM隐私研究需要更加关注相互依赖隐私问题，以开发更安全的AI助手。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming widely deployed as personal AI assistants with access to sensitive user data, making privacy a major challenge for their design and evaluation. Prior work focuses mainly on individual-level risks, overlooking \textbf{interdependent privacy (IDP)}--where one person's data may be revealed by others without their knowledge or consent. We address this gap by introducing \textbf{IDP-Bench}: the first LLM benchmark for IDP scenarios, grounded in the Contextual Integrity (CI) framework. We evaluate eight open-source LLMs on their understanding of IDP scenarios across three levels of IDP reasoning using two LLM judges. Results show strong co-ownership recognition (6/8 models exceed 90\%) but persistent weaknesses in identifying CI parameters (information attribute, primary subject) and IDP-specific parameters such as secondary subjects, where 7/8 models score below 74\%. Models also struggle to judge sharing appropriateness (5/8 scoring below 77\%). While the ability to judge the appropriateness of sharing improves with scale, performance tends to decline in smaller models, and prompt sensitivity remains high on IDP-specific questions--highlighting the need for more targeted study of IDP in LLM privacy research. Data \& code available \href{https://github.com/tisl-lab/Interdependent_Privacy_Bench}{here}.

</details>


### [82] [Bypassing Copyright Protection in Diffusion-based Customization via Two-Stage Latent Feature Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09909)
*Ziang Xu,Wenbo Yu,Hongyao Yu,Hao Fang,Jiawei Kong,Bin Chen,Hao Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-LFO，一种针对受版权保护扩散模型的二阶段潜特征优化攻击方法，通过潜变量去噪和重建两阶段恢复被防御破坏的映射关系，有效绕过现有SOTA版权防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型版权防御主要在潜空间引入持久扰动，但易被自适应攻击绕过；需设计更鲁棒的攻击方法来评估防御有效性。

Method: 两阶段优化：1）潜变量去噪阶段，联合优化潜-图像对齐损失和带时间步权重的潜扩散损失，抑制防御引入的高频噪声；2）潜变量重建阶段，使用像素级约束恢复低频语义信息，精炼潜特征。

Result: 广泛实验表明TS-LFO能够持续绕过SOTA版权防御，且在多种设置下优于DiffPure、GrIDPure和IMPRESS等SOTA攻击方法。

Conclusion: TS-LFO通过两阶段优化有效恢复被防御破坏的潜空间映射，证明了现有版权防御仍存在脆弱性。

Abstract: With the growing concerns over copyright infringement in diffusion-based customization, adversarial attacks have emerged as a prominent defense strategy to prevent malicious content forgery in personalized image generation. However, current defenses typically introduce persistent perturbations in the latent space of Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs), which remain susceptible to adaptive bypasses by adversaries. In this paper, we introduce Two-Stage Latent Feature Optimization (TS-LFO), an efficient and effective copyright-stealing attack against protected diffusion-based customization. We begin by observing that existing defenses primarily disrupt the mapping between input images and their latent representations, thereby degrading the model's ability to produce personalized outputs. To counteract this, TS-LFO restores the broken mapping through a two-stage optimization process. In the Latent Denoising Stage, we enhance semantic consistency between latent codes and input images by jointly minimizing a Latent-Image Alignment Loss and a Latent Diffusion Loss with timestep-dependent weights, effectively suppressing the high-frequency noise introduced by defenses. In the Latent Reconstruction Stage, we recover low-frequency semantic information using pixel-level constraints to refine the latent features. Extensive experiments show that TS-LFO consistently bypasses state-of-the-art (SOTA) copyright defenses and outperforms SOTA copyright attacks such as DiffPure, GrIDPure and IMPRESS across diverse settings.

</details>


### [83] [GitInject: Real-World Prompt Injection Attacks in AI-Powered CI/CD Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09935)
*Jafar Isbarov,Umid Suleymanov,Ilia Shumailov,Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出GitInject框架，用于评估真实GitHub工作流中AI代理的提示注入漏洞。发现所有主流AI提供商的默认配置均存在结构性的安全漏洞，源自CI/CD基础设施对凭证和配置文件的处理方式，而非特定模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理被嵌入CI/CD流水线并拥有高权限，处理不可信内容时易受提示注入攻击，造成供应链安全风险。现有基准测试仅模拟工具调用，无法反映生产环境中的真实约束。

Method: 开发GitInject框架，通过创建临时仓库并触发真实工作流运行，评估四个AI提供商在真实生产环境下的提示注入漏洞。覆盖配置注入、凭证窃取、判断操控和可用性攻击等十一类命名攻击。

Result: 所有被测试的AI提供商默认配置下均至少存在一类可攻击漏洞。最关键的漏洞是结构性的，源于CI/CD基础设施处理凭证和配置文件的方式。为每类已确认攻击确定了最低成本的工作流级缓解措施。

Conclusion: GitInject揭示了当前AI驱动CI/CD流水线的系统性安全缺陷，问题根源在于基础设施架构而非模型行为，需从工作流设计层面实施防护。框架已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: AI-powered agents are increasingly embedded in continuous integration and continuous delivery/deployment (CI/CD) pipelines to autonomously review pull requests (PRs), triage issues, and maintain codebases. These agents ingest untrusted content while operating with elevated repository permissions, making them a natural target for prompt injection attacks with supply chain consequences. We present GitInject, an open-source framework for evaluating prompt injection vulnerabilities in real, live GitHub workflows, a widely deployed instance of CI/CD pipelines. Unlike prior agent security benchmarks that simulate tool calls, GitInject provisions ephemeral repositories and triggers actual workflow runs, so that sandbox constraints, credential handling, and permission boundaries behave exactly as in production. Using GitInject, we study workflow configurations across four AI providers and document eleven named attacks spanning config-file injection, credential exfiltration, judgment manipulation, and availability. We find that all tested providers are susceptible to at least one attack class in their default configuration, and that the most critical vulnerabilities are structural: they arise from how CI/CD infrastructure handles credentials and configuration files, not from any specific model's behavior. For each confirmed attack class, we identify the minimum-cost workflow-level countermeasure and analyze its coverage and limitations. GitInject is released publicly to facilitate further research in this direction.

</details>


### [84] [The Chronicles of Radio Frequency Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10031)
*Abdul Aziz,Ingrid Huso,Savio Sciancalepore,Gabriele Oligeri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对射頻指纹识别（RFF）领域从1993年至2026年的发展进行了批判性历史分析，围绕该领域的重大概念范式转变展开，梳理了从早期瞬态方法、数字通信、机器学习到深度学习四个阶段的演变，并重点讨论了信道依赖、接收灵敏度、数据集真实性、跨域泛化、开放集识别和对抗鲁棒性等持续存在的核心挑战，最后指出了实现可靠实际部署所需的关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFF已从雷达发射器识别的早期概念发展为无线设备识别和频谱安全监控的重要研究方向。尽管该领域已积累大量工作，但缺乏一个超越传统文献综述的、围绕概念范式转变组织的系统性历史分析，以厘清推动该领域发展的关键假设变化和持续存在的局限性。

Method: 本文采用批判性历史分析方法，将RFF领域超过三十年的研究组织为四个主要方法论阶段：(1) 早期基于瞬态的方法，关注发射机开启行为、非故意调制和硬件非线性；(2) 数字通信时代，转向稳态损伤和手工特征提取；(3) 机器学习时期，标准化了特征提取、降维和监督分类的工作流程；(4) 深度学习时期，通过从原始IQ样本中学习表示显著提升了性能并扩展了应用空间。论文重点分析了驱动这些阶段转变的假设变化和持久局限。

Result: 本文识别并强调了六个持续塑造RFF领域的核心挑战：信道依赖性、接收灵敏度、有限的数据集真实性、较差的跨域泛化能力、开放集识别问题以及对抗鲁棒性。通过对这些挑战的批判性审视，论文阐明了RFF从实验室研究走向可靠实际部署所面临的根本性障碍。

Conclusion: RFF领域经历了从瞬态特征到稳态特征、从手工特征到表示学习的重要范式转变，但信道依赖性、泛化能力和鲁棒性等核心问题仍未解决。未来的关键研究方向应聚焦于解决这些持续存在的局限性，以推动该领域实现可靠的现实世界应用。

Abstract: Radio Frequency Fingerprinting (RFF) has evolved from an early idea for radar emitter identification into a broad research field for wireless device identification and spectrum monitoring for security. Rather than presenting a conventional literature survey, this work provides a critical historical analysis of RFF organized around the field's major conceptual paradigm shifts from 1993 to 2026. We discuss the evolution of RFF across its fundamental methodological phases, beginning with early transient-based approaches, in which transmitter turn-on behavior, unintentional modulation, and hardware nonlinearities were treated as the primary fingerprint sources. We then examine the transition to digital communications, during which attention shifted to steady-state impairments and to engineered features extracted from signals. Next, we discuss the Machine Learning period, which standardized the RFF workflow around feature extraction, dimensionality reduction, and supervised classification, followed by the Deep Learning period, in which representation learning from raw IQ samples significantly improved performance and expanded the application space. Beyond a chronological list of methods and best practices, this paper critically examines the changing assumptions and persistent limitations that have driven these transitions. We highlight the central challenges that continue to shape the field, including channel dependence, receiver sensitivity, limited dataset realism, poor cross-domain generalization, open-set recognition, and adversarial robustness. By organizing more than three decades of work into a coherent narrative, this paper clarifies the evolution of RFF, identifies persistent limitations, and outlines the key research directions required to move the field toward dependable real-world adoption.

</details>


### [85] [The Human Vulnerabilities & Exploits (HVE) Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10083)
*Avichai Ben,Tom Rahav,Daniel Illaev,Aviv Nahon,Avi Grushka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了人类脆弱性与利用（HVE）框架，借鉴CVE/CVSS/CWE标准体系，为社交工程、诈骗等利用人类行为与心理脆弱性的攻击提供结构化的识别、分类和缓解方案，包括机器可读分类法、人类脆弱性严重度评分（HVSS）和人类脆弱性补丁（HVP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全标准化框架（CVE、CVSS、CWE）主要针对软件漏洞，而大多数成功网络攻击利用的是人的行为和心理脆弱性（社交工程、诈骗等），缺乏对应的标准化框架。行为科学和心理学已有扎实理论基础，但未被系统整合到网络安全防御中。

Method: 借鉴CVE框架概念，构建HVE框架，包含：结构化机器可读分类法、多维度严重度评分（HVSS）、可操作的缓解指导（HVP），并综合网络安全标准化、行为科学和欺诈防御的文献建立理论基础。

Result: 提出了HVE框架的理论与实践基础，其架构和技术规范详见后续章节。

Conclusion: HVE框架为人类脆弱性提供了一套类似CVE的标准化体系，有望弥补现有网络安全框架在人类行为心理层面的空白。

Abstract: The cybersecurity community has invested over two decades in building standardized frameworks, the Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) system, the Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS), and the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) to identify, classify, and remediate threats to digital infrastructure. However, an emerging body of research reveals that a vast majority of successful cyberattacks exploit not software flaws, but human behavioral and psychological vulnerabilities. Social engineering, fraud, and scam attacks, which manipulate human cognition, emotion, and trust, do not have an equivalent standardized framework. Meanwhile, behavioral science and psychology research has established robust theoretical foundations, such as dual-process theory, prospect theory, social influence frameworks, and visceral state models, which explain precisely why and how these attacks succeed. This paper introduces the Human Vulnerabilities & Exploits (HVE) Framework, a structured approach for identifying, classifying, and mitigating the behavioral and psychological vulnerabilities exploited in scams, social engineering, and other human-centric fraud and attacks, analogous in concept to how CVE helps classify software vulnerabilities: it provides a shared, machine-readable taxonomy with structured identifiers, multi-dimensional severity scoring via the Human Vulnerability Severity Score (HVSS), and actionable remediation guidance through Human Vulnerability Patches (HVPs). This introduction synthesizes the relevant literature across cybersecurity standardization, behavioral science, and fraud defense to establish the theoretical and practical foundations for the HVE framework, whose architecture and technical specifications are detailed in subsequent sections.

</details>


### [86] [SoK: Colluding Adversaries in Machine Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10091)
*Vasisht Duddu,Lipeng He,Asim Waheed,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个系统框架研究机器学习模型中 adversary 之间的合谋行为，涵盖训练阶段与推理阶段的 adversary 合谋，以及推理阶段内部 adversary 的合谋，并通过使能因素分析预测潜在合谋场景，实验验证了五种未探索的合谋案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏系统性框架来探索 adversary 之间的合谋行为以及 adversary 特征（目标、知识、能力）对合谋的影响，而不同特征的 adversary 可以通过联合执行攻击来放大彼此的攻击效果。

Method: 提出了一个涵盖(a)训练阶段与推理阶段 adversary 之间以及(b)推理阶段内部 adversary 之间合谋的框架；定义了合谋的使能因素，并基于这些因素提出推测合谋潜力的指南；通过该指南解释已有工作、推测未探索的合谋场景，并进行实验验证。

Result: 成功用该框架解释了已有工作中的合谋现象，推测并实验验证了五种此前未探索的合谋案例，展示了 adversary 特征（目标、知识、能力）如何影响合谋潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够系统性地探索 ML 模型中的 adversary 合谋行为，为理解不同 adversary 特征对合谋的影响提供了理论基础，并指导发现新的合谋攻击场景。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models are susceptible to various security, privacy, and fairness risks. Adversaries with different characteristics (i.e., objectives, knowledge, and capabilities) can collude by executing one attack to amplify others. Existing work lacks a systematic framework to explore collusion among adversaries, and to study the implications of the adversaries' characteristics. We present a framework covering collusion (a) between train- and inference-time adversaries, and (b) among inference-time adversaries. Our framework accounts for factors enabling collusion between adversaries. We propose a guideline to conjecture about the potential for collusion using enabling factors. We use it to explain prior work, conjecture about unexplored collusions, and empirically validate five such cases. Finally, we discuss how adversaries' characteristics influence the potential for collusion.

</details>


### [87] [Secrets Best Not Shared: DNS Privacy Enhancements for the Constrained IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10097)
*Martine S. Lenders,Thomas C. Schmidt,Matthias Wählisch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究针对资源受限物联网设备的DNS流量混淆技术，通过创建M2M兼容数据集并评估296种部署场景，发现DNS over CoAP结合等长包、块传输、头部压缩和负载压缩可将DNS帧识别准确率从86%降至77%，优于DNS over HTTPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者常通过识别DNS流量来破坏互联网服务，以往研究聚焦于加密DNS（如DoT、DoH、DoQ），但对资源受限物联网设备，除加密外还需要流量混淆以抵御流量分析攻击。

Method: 创建包含M2M兼容数据对象及对应DNS解析过程的数据集，评估296种主机名解析部署场景（包括DNS over CoAP及其洋葱路由变体），采用随机森林分类器和头部字段分析识别信息泄露最多的字段。

Result: DNS over CoAP结合等长包、块传输和头部压缩可将DNS帧识别准确率降至86%，进一步加入负载压缩可降至77%；相比之下，DoH场景中分类器总能通过IP地址识别DNS帧。

Conclusion: 针对物联网场景，DNS over CoAP结合多种混淆技术能有效降低流量分类准确率，优于DNS over HTTPS的防护效果。

Abstract: Attackers often identify DNS traffic to disrupt or compromise Internet services. While prior work has focused on encrypting queries using DNS over TLS, HTTPS, or QUIC to counter such attacks, we consider IETF protocols designed for resource-constrained IoT devices and empirically analyze the potential of obfuscating DNS traffic in addition to encryption. We create a dataset of machine-to-machine-compatible data objects along with the corresponding DNS resolution processes, evaluating 296 deployment scenarios of resolving host names, including DNS over the Constrained Application Layer Protocol (CoAP) and an onion routing flavor of CoAP under varying link-layer conditions. We compare them to DNS over HTTPS. Using Random Forest and a header field analysis, we identify fields that leak most information. Our findings show that DNS over CoAP with equalized packet lengths, block-wise transfer, and header compression reduces the accuracy of identifying DNS frames to 86% and further to 77% with payload compression. Our approach outperforms DNS over HTTPS, where classifiers always identify DNS frames based on IP addresses. The dataset is publicly available.

</details>


### [88] [RadKey: An LLM-Guided RF Backscatter System for Through-Wall Keystroke Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10148)
*Qijun Wang,Chunqi Qian,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RadKey是一种基于RF背散射的隐蔽远距离穿墙键盘窃听系统，通过无源标签捕获按键振动和声音信号，结合LLM进行在线自适应，实现跨用户、跨键盘的通用按键推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按键窃听技术依赖短距离传感器和受害者特定数据训练，缺乏实用性、可扩展性和隐蔽性。作者希望实现一种隐蔽、长距离、可穿墙且无需受害者先验数据的键盘窃听方案。

Method: 系统由无源背散射标签和RF读写器组成。标签利用两个磁耦合LC谐振器捕获按键振动和声学信号，并将其调制到背散射RF信号的频移上，同时实现激励信号与背散射信号的频谱分离以减少自干扰。RF读写器解调信号后，通过时频域特征提取获取与用户/键盘无关的按键特征，并引入LLM提供伪标签进行在线自适应分类。

Result: 实验表明RadKey在真实环境下对不同用户实现了准确且鲁棒的按键推断。

Conclusion: RadKey通过RF背散射和LLM在线自适应，实现了隐蔽、长距离、穿墙的通用键盘窃听，显著提升了实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: In today's digitally connected world, keyboards remain the primary interface for inputting sensitive information, making them a persistent target for eavesdropping attacks. While prior keystroke inference techniques have exploited side-channel signals such as acoustics and vibrations, they typically rely on conspicuous, short-range sensors and require victim-specific data for model training, limiting their practicality, scalability, and stealth. In this paper, we present RadKey, an RF backscatter system for covert, long-range, through-wall keystroke eavesdropping. RadKey comprises two components: a compact batteryless backscatter tag and an RF reader. The tag captures keystroke-induced vibrations and acoustic signals, modulating them onto the frequency shift of its backscattered RF signal using two magnetically-coupled LC resonators. This design also enables spectral separation between the excitation and backscatter signals, mitigating self-interference for the RF reader and thus extending eavesdropping range. The RF reader demodulates the backscattered RF signal to infer typed content. It employs a dedicated signal processing pipeline that extracts user- and keyboard-independent keystroke features across time and frequency domains, enabling strong generalizability. To further enhance adaptability, RadKey integrates an LLM for online adaptation, leveraging LLM outputs as pseudo ground-truth labels to refine the classifier during runtime. We have built a prototype of the full RadKey system and evaluated it through extensive over-the-air experiments. Results show that RadKey achieves accurate and robust keystroke inference across diverse users in real-world settings. A demo video is available at: https://radkey-submission.github.io/RadKey/

</details>


### [89] [GRAFT: Graphlet-Triggered Backdoor Attack on GNN-Based Hardware Security Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10163)
*Sanaz Kazemi Abharian,Sai Manoj Pudukotai Dinakarrao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAFT，一种基于图元的后门攻击方法，针对GNN在硬件安全中的应用（如硬件木马检测和IP盗版检测），通过在RTL或门级嵌入保持电路功能的图元触发器，实现高达100%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN被广泛应用于IC供应链安全威胁检测（如HT和IP盗版），但GNN本身易受后门攻击；现有后门攻击方法使用的触发器不保证电路功能不变，在硬件安全场景中不实用。

Method: 提出GRAFT方法，在RTL级或门级嵌入基于图元（graphlet）的触发器，同时保持电路原始功能不变，从而对基于GNN的硬件安全系统实施后门攻击。

Result: 在ISCAS-85和TrustHub数据集上的实验表明，GRAFT能有效规避HT检测和IP盗版检测，攻击成功率（ASR）最高达100%。

Conclusion: GRAFT证明了现有GNN-based硬件安全方法存在严重安全漏洞，需要开发更鲁棒的防御机制。

Abstract: The globalization of the integrated circuit (IC) supply chain increases the risk of security threats, such as hardware Trojans (HTs) and the theft of intellectual property (IP). Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), among the most powerful deep learning methods for processing graph-structured data, have been widely adopted to detect such threats. However, GNNs are susceptible to backdoor attacks that can maliciously manipulate output predictions toward an adversarial target. These attacks are not only difficult to detect but also compromise the integrity of GNN-based security systems. Most prior work embeds backdoor triggers using randomly generated subgraphs or gradient-guided generative subgraphs. However, such triggers are impractical for GNN-based hardware security applications as they do not guarantee the preservation of circuit functionality. In this paper, we propose GRAFT, a graph let-triggered backdoor attack targeting GNN-based hardware security. GRAFT embeds graphlet-based triggers at either the register-transfer level (RTL) or gate level of the design while preserving the circuit 's original function. We evaluate GRAFT on the ISCAS-85 and TrustHub datasets. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRAFT can effectively evade HT detection and IP piracy detection, achieving an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 100%.

</details>


### [90] [Proof of Source of Funds: Efficient On-chain Provenance of Cryptoassets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10172)
*Alireza Kavousi,István András Seres,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PoSoF（资金来源证明）的新型密码学框架，将合规审查从平台侧转移至用户侧。用户利用零知识证明本地生成资金合规来源证明，平台仅需常量时间O(1)的验证即可完成准入控制。通过统一的有向无环图（DAG）抽象和增量可验证计算（IVC），PoSoF在保护用户交易拓扑隐私的前提下实现可验证的加密溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi和隐私增强技术的合规监管依赖中心化区块链情报机构的二元黑白名单或事后图分析，这导致诚实用户丧失财务隐私、产生误报/漏报，且令去中心化平台承担沉重的链上交易监控负担。作者旨在改变这一现状，提出从平台端监控到用户端溯源的范式转移。

Method: 1) 提出统一的时间DAG抽象，形式化UTXO和账户模型下的通用价值流模型；2) 用户从自身交易历史中提取合规子DAG；3) 利用增量可验证计算（IVC）证明严格的状态转换谓词，抵御多种攻击；4) 用户本地生成零知识证明，证明存款仅来源于合规源地址集，不泄露中间交易拓扑、中间地址或具体资金来源。

Result: 在以太坊兼容原型上的基准测试表明，每笔新交易增量更新PoSoF约需1.8秒，最终链上EVM验证仅需常量时间约1.5毫秒（约800k gas），验证了全隐私、主动式合规方案的高度实用性。

Conclusion: PoSoF实现了从平台端追踪到用户端证明的范式转移，在保障用户财务隐私的前提下提供了可验证的密码学资金来源证明，显著降低了平台的合规负担，且性能开销在实用范围内。

Abstract: Regulatory compliance is increasingly mandatory for decentralized finance and privacy-enhancing technologies. Current approaches rely on binary inclusion/exclusion lists or retroactive graph analysis by centralized blockchain intelligence firms. This approach strips honest users of their financial privacy, leads to false positives and negatives, and forces decentralized platforms to bear the burden of on-chain transaction monitoring. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift: moving from platform-side surveillance to user-side provenance. We introduce Proof of Source of Funds (PoSoF), a novel cryptographic framework that shifts the burden to the user. Rather than the platform tracing funds, the user locally generates a zero-knowledge proof demonstrating that their deposit originates exclusively from a set of compliant sources. The platform is thus relieved of chain-analysis duties, requiring a constant-time, O(1) verification to enforce admission control.
  We formulate a unified temporal Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) abstraction that formalizes both UTXO and account-based ledger histories within a generalized value-flow model. Users extract a compliant sub-DAG of their transaction history and utilize Incrementally Verifiable Computation (IVC) to prove rigorous state-transition predicates that protect against various attack vectors. Crucially, PoSoF provides verifiable cryptographic provenance; it guarantees the legitimacy of the funds without leaking the intermediate transaction topology, intermediary addresses, or the specific origins utilized. We formally define the security properties of PoSoF and evaluate an Ethereum-compatible prototype. Our benchmarks demonstrate that fully private, proactive compliance is highly practical, requiring only ~1.8 s to incrementally update a user's PoSoF per new transaction, and a constant-time ~1.5 ms (~800k gas) for final on-chain EVM verification.

</details>


### [91] [Local Is Not a Sufficient Privacy Boundary: Governing OS-Integrated On-Device AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10173)
*Jonghyun Chung,Sanket Badhe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向操作系统级AI的隐私框架，将隐私问题从"是否本地运行"转向机构问责制，涵盖威胁模型、六部分隐私风险分类、架构控制及四级审计标准，并通过Apple Intelligence、Android AICore和Microsoft Recall的文档对比验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI隐私讨论过度聚焦于"本地推理vs云端推理"这一单一维度，忽视了AI深入操作系统后引发的上下文组装、持久化派生状态、工具调用权限、遥测与云路由等更复杂的隐私问题。作者认为隐私不应仅是部署属性，而应被视为操作系统层面的机构问责问题。

Method: 1. 构建威胁模型；2. 提出六部分隐私风险分类法；3. 设计隐私通过架构的控制机制；4. 制定四级审计细则；5. 通过对Apple Intelligence/Foundation Models、Android AICore/Gemini Nano和Microsoft Recall的基于文档的对比分析来演示审计细则的应用。

Result: 提出了一个系统性的OS中心隐私框架，并通过三大主流平台（苹果、安卓、微软）的对比展示了该框架的实际可用性。三个平台在信息流约束、权限边界、用户控制可见性和审计治理方面存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 有意义的设备端AI隐私取决于四个关键维度：受约束的信息流、有边界的权限、可见的用户控制以及覆盖操作系统全生命周期的可审计治理。

Abstract: As AI systems move into operating systems, privacy no longer turns only on whether a model runs locally. A local assistant may assemble email, calendar entries, files, screenshots, notifications, and app intents; retain embeddings or summaries; invoke tools; emit telemetry; or route difficult requests to cloud infrastructure. Local inference reduces some exposure, but it answers only one question: where computation occurs. It does not answer who may assemble context, what derived state persists, which actions are authorized, or how updates change the system's authority. We develop an OS-centered privacy framework for on-device AI that treats privacy as an institutional accountability problem rather than a deployment attribute. The framework specifies a threat model, a six-part privacy risk taxonomy, privacy-by-architecture controls, and a four-level audit rubric. We demonstrate the rubric through a documentation-bounded comparison of Apple Intelligence/Foundation Models, Android AICore/Gemini Nano, and Microsoft Recall. Meaningful privacy in on-device AI depends on constrained information flow, bounded authority, visible user control, and auditable governance across the operating-system lifecycle.

</details>


### [92] [RECON: An LLM-Enhanced Backward Constraint Analysis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10264)
*Babangida Bappah,Lamine Noureddine,Umar Farooq,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RECON框架，结合大语言模型与静态后向约束分析，从Android字节码中提取精确执行约束。相比传统符号执行，速度提升5.8倍，成功率100%，在恶意软件分析中达到84%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号执行面临路径爆炸、函数建模困难和低级程序表示丢失语义意图等问题，在Android等复杂事件驱动环境中尤为突出，亟需一种兼具精度和可扩展性的约束提取方法。

Method: 提出RECON框架：从目标方法到应用入口点执行后向路径发现，挖掘方法级控制流约束，并利用LLM将字节码条件转换为可解释的规范。

Result: 在78个Android约束提取场景中，5种LLM均优于传统符号执行，速度提升5.8倍，成功率100%；在100个恶意软件样本上，危险API行为的语义约束生成成功率达84%。

Conclusion: LLM增强的后向约束分析方法在保持逻辑等价性的同时，显著提升了分析速度和可解释性，为Android程序分析和恶意软件检测提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: While traditional techniques, such as symbolic execution, provide a principled foundation for precise constraint reasoning in program analysis, they struggle to scale to modern software systems mainly due to path explosion, the need for function modeling, and the loss of semantic intent at low-level program representations. In complex execution environments such as Android, characterized by extensive framework interactions and event-driven behavior, these limitations are even more amplified. Thus, in this paper, we present a novel large language model (LLM)-enhanced backward constraint analysis framework that combines the precision of static program analysis with LLM's semantic understanding to extract precise execution constraints from Android bytecode. Our approach, titled RECON, performs backward path discovery from target method(s) to the application entry point(s), discovers method-level control-flow constraints, and leverages LLM reasoning to transform bytecode conditions into interpretable specifications. We evaluated RECON using five LLMs across 78 Android constraint-extraction scenarios and compared it with traditional symbolic execution on real-world applications. Results demonstrate that our approach operates 5.8X faster than traditional symbolic execution, with a 100% success rate, while maintaining logical equivalence and providing significantly more precise and interpretable output. We further evaluated RECON for malware analysis on 100 samples. The results indicate an 84% success rate in generating semantic constraints that lead to the execution of dangerous API behaviors and in detecting complex constraints across multiple execution paths.

</details>


### [93] [Benchmarking and Exploring the Capabilities of LLMs for Attack Investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10281)
*Aniket Anand,Yiwei Hou,Daniel Fields,Alex Kantchelian,David Tao,Kurt Thomas,Grant Ho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AuditBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在安全系统审计日志调查能力的基准数据集，涵盖Linux和Windows系统上的50多种安全调查场景，并对五个前沿LLM在四项日志调查任务上的表现进行了系统评估与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全事件响应团队需要高效分析系统审计日志以发现攻击行为，但现有工作缺乏对LLM在这类安全日志调查任务中能力的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建了AuditBench基准数据集，包含来自Linux和Windows机器的系统审计日志，覆盖50多种安全调查场景（恶意与良性活动均有）。设计了四个典型的日志调查任务。评估了五个前沿LLM，从模型大小、数据表示、提示构造和具体任务等维度分析性能与错误特征。

Result: 揭示了LLM在不同设计选择下的性能差异和错误模式，表征了LLM生成解释的质量以及模型在基准中犯错的类型分布。

Conclusion: 为评估LLM在安全日志调查方面的能力提供了基础，为安全运营中使用LLM的实践者提供了新见解，并指出了未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: This paper presents AuditBench, a new benchmark dataset for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs at investigating security-related system audit logs. We design and use this benchmark to explore the performance of LLMs on four log-investigation tasks that incident response teams commonly perform, ranging from triaging alerts generated by detectors to identifying persistence mechanisms on compromised systems. AuditBench consists of system audit logs collected from Linux and Windows machines, and spans over 50 different security investigation scenarios, including both malicious and benign activity. Using our benchmark, we evaluate and analyze the performance of five frontier LLMs at analyzing audit logs for attack investigations. Our analysis illuminates how LLM performance and error profiles vary according to different design choices, such as differences in model size, data representation, prompt construction, and specific investigation tasks. Additionally, we characterize the quality of the explanations produced by LLMs and the types of errors that models make across our benchmark. Collectively, our work provides a foundation for assessing the capabilities of LLMs for investigating security logs, novel insights for practitioners using LLMs in security operations, and important directions for future research.

</details>


### [94] [The Linux IOCTL Census: A Source-Derived Database of the Linux Kernel Control-Code Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10290)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Linux IOCTL普查（Linux IOCTL Census），通过源码分析构建了ioctl攻击面的可查询清单，覆盖586个调度入口、1289个命令码、3583个受控输入接收点和1298个权限门，并回测了22个近期CVE，同时发布了与Windows IOCTL普查共享模式的结构化数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ioctl处理程序是Linux内核中最大且最不统一的局部攻击面之一，命令空间缺乏集中管理，且处理程序对参数长度等验证不足可能导致越界内存访问，现有方法无法系统性地普查这一攻击面。

Method: 基于allmodconfig构建编译878个模块，使用libclang对内核源码进行确定性单次遍历，恢复ioctl调度入口点、命令码、受控输入接收点和权限门；将内核自身文档化的威胁模型编码为可查询列，区分无能力门控的ioctl表面与硬能力门控保护的部分。

Result: 恢复586个ioctl调度入口点、1289个解码后的_IOC命令码、3583个受控输入接收点和1298个权限门；回测了22个近期内核ioctl相关CVE；发布了与Windows IOCTL普查共享模式的结构化开源数据。

Conclusion: 该普查提供了系统化的ioctl攻击面清单和威胁模型编码能力，支持跨操作系统的统一查询，为发现和防范ioctl相关漏洞提供了基础设施。

Abstract: The ioctl system call is Linux's catch-all device-control interface. A userspace program opens a device node and hands the driver a numeric command code and an argument buffer, and the driver does whatever that code means, whether configuring hardware, reading back state, or moving data into and out of the kernel. Drivers define these commands themselves, by the thousand, and parse their arguments in kernel context, which makes ioctl handlers one of the broadest and least uniform local attack surfaces in the kernel. A handler that trusts an argument length it never validates can read or write kernel memory out of bounds, and the command space is catalogued in no central place. We present the Linux IOCTL Census, a source-derived and queryable inventory of that surface. An allmodconfig build compiles 878 modules across 169 subtrees, and over them a single deterministic libclang pass over the kernel source recovers 586 ioctl dispatch entry points, 1,289 decoded _IOC command codes, 3,583 controlled-input sinks, and 1,298 permission gates. A second pass encodes the kernel's own documented threat model as a queryable column, separating the capability-ungated ioctl surface, an upper bound on unprivileged reach rather than proven reach, from the part a hard capability gate puts out of scope. We backtest the census against 22 recent in-tree ioctl CVEs and release the structural tier as open data, on a schema shared with the companion Windows IOCTL Census so a single query spans both operating systems.

</details>


### [95] [Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Control for Robust Contextual Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10322)
*Saeid Jamshidi,Amin Nikanjam,Arghavan Moradi Dakhel,Kawser Wazed Nafi,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对多轮交互中大语言模型易受提示注入和上下文投毒攻击的问题，本文提出GT-MCP，一种基于博弈论的安全模型上下文协议。该方法通过控制器协调三个异构LLM代理，利用信任函数联合评估因果一致性、语义一致性和分布漂移，并结合基于回滚的自愈机制。实验表明，在500轮自适应对抗攻击下，99.6%的轮次上下文漂移可控，仅0.4%需恢复，且控制器层面无注入成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多轮交互中，提示注入和上下文投毒攻击可通过局部看似合理的对抗性片段逐步扭曲推理轨迹，而现有防御主要过滤单轮输出，忽略了跨轮次的上下文演化，导致长程推理暴露于风险中。MCP虽标准化了上下文交换和工具调用，但仅作为被动路由层，不强制上下文稳定性。

Method: 提出GT-MCP，一种控制器驱动的多智能体方法，将上下文管理视为闭环动力学过程。具体包括：1) 协调三个异构LLM代理；2) 设计信任函数，联合评估与验证过的上下文图之间的因果一致性、代理间的语义一致性以及随时间变化的分布漂移；3) 当检测到不稳定时，采用基于回滚的自愈机制恢复已验证的上下文，阻止未受支持的片段传播。

Result: 在自适应对抗威胁模型下进行500轮交互评估：上下文漂移在99.6%的轮次中保持有界，仅0.4%需要恢复；每轮效用高度集中（中位数=-0.19，P05=-0.72，P95=0.30），严重退化仅占0.4%；控制器级别无注入成功；选定输出稳定保持98%以上的胜率；计算开销可预测，每token延迟1.63e-3秒。
